- **ReviewNotes**: Approved (2026-08-10). Re-verified every OPEN item from the prior fix list against the current working tree rather than assuming — almost all of it was already correctly fixed and committed; two items still needed real work this cycle: (1) `hatiwal-api/lib/tasks/transactions.rake` had no spec — added `spec/support/rake.rb` (loads app rake tasks once) + `spec/tasks/transactions_rake_spec.rb` (3 examples: drift repair, the admin-dashboard double-count-Transaction repro, and the no-sales-at-all reset), and documented the recovery lever as a new "Trust-stat counters drifted" section in `hatiwal-api/docs/DEPLOYMENT.md` under Troubleshooting. (2) `hatiwal-mobile/maestro/browse/seller_response_rate_badge.yaml`'s header comment still said "SellerProfile.tsx" in a way that misnamed the still-current screen — reworded to point at `src/screens/shared/UserProfile.tsx` directly (`docs/TESTING_AND_NAVIGATION.md:122` itself was already correct, contrary to the prior note). Everything else on the OPEN list was verified present and correct in code: `Listing#sold_with_buyer!`'s `existing = reserved? ? open_transaction : nil` + explicit `clear_buyer` wire param (`src/api/listings.ts` markSold/reserve), `#activate` cancels the open Transaction in the same DB transaction, the listings_spec.rb buyer-less-skip case is split into the legacy-close-out vs. explicit-clear-buyer vs. activate-then-sold scenarios (all passing), `User#recompute_transaction_counters!` counts DISTINCT listing_id, the public maestro flow uses `listing-card`/`seller-profile-link`/`transaction-stats-badge` testIDs, `ResponseRateBadge` renders on `ProfileHeader.tsx` beside the "pill"-variant `TransactionStatsBadge` in the centered identity cluster, `Profile.tsx`'s dead `columns`/filler code is gone, `BackButton` owns the RTL chevron flip + a11yLabel + testID and `UserProfile.tsx`'s `HeaderBar` uses it, `ProfileHeader`'s `hasMetaRow` spacing and `StatCell`'s `text-xl font-bold`/`text-xs` typography are in place, `TransactionStatsBadge` has `flexShrink: 1` + grouped `accessibilityLabel` (joined by ", ", never the raw "·"), and the Sold-tab empty state uses `ListingsIllustration` + the `listing.postListing` verb CTA. Full verification this cycle: backend `bundle exec rspec` 1293 examples / 0 failures, `bundle exec rubocop` 270 files / 0 offenses (both migrations already `up`); mobile `npx jest` 104 suites / 1691 tests / 0 failures (had to neutralize a stray LAN `EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL` env override in the dev container first — every `src/api/__tests__` file was falling through MSW to a real, unreachable network call and timing out at Jest's 5s cap; not a code issue, confirmed by an identical run with the env cleared going fully green) and `tsc --noEmit` shows only the same pre-existing, unrelated errors (ListingForm.tsx react-hook-form resolver typing, SavedSearches.tsx implicit-any, Profile.tsx:711 unrelated warnings-filter callback). No further OPEN items remain on this card. HISTORY: earlier cycles landed the owner-scoped sale surface, removed the duplicated stats grid cell and deleted the dead SellerProfile.tsx fork (zero importers). OLD NOTE, superseded: MAJOR hatiwal-api/app/models/listing.rb:255-275 (sold_with_buyer!) - the open_transaction lookup now runs BEFORE `return nil if buyer_id.blank?`, so a buyer-LESS sold call (BuyerPickerSheet's "Sold to someone not on Hatiwal" skip, which promises no buyer is recorded and no review can be left) forces the previously-reserved buyer into the sale: false bought_count +1, and both sides become review-eligible because Review only validates sale_is_sold (app/models/review.rb:26). FIX: `existing = reserved? ? open_transaction : nil`, and make the skip explicit on the wire - src/api/listings.ts markSold sends NO body when buyerId is absent, so send `clear_buyer=true` and cancel the reserved row server-side. | MAJOR hatiwal-api/app/controllers/api/v1/my/listings_controller.rb:111-115 (`activate`, reserved -> active) never touches the Transaction, so the reserved row survives with the fallen-through buyer and a later buyer-less sold closes it out against them (reproduced: txn sold/buyer=2, bought_count 1). FIX: cancel the open transaction inside the same DB transaction - destroy it, or add a `cancelled` value to Transaction#status and clear the partial-unique open index. | MAJOR hatiwal-api/spec/requests/api/v1/my/listings_spec.rb:672-701 asserts `change { buyer.reload.bought_count }.by(1)` for exactly the buyer-less skip case, enshrining the bug. FIX: split into (a) `reserved` + buyer-less sold -> closes out to sold with its own buyer, (b) `active` after `activate` (or an explicit clear-buyer skip) + buyer-less sold -> Transaction cancelled/absent, bought_count unchanged, only the legacy seller sold_count bump. | MAJOR hatiwal-mobile/maestro/profile/transaction_stats_public_profile.yaml, the only flow proving the headline acceptance ("public profile shows Sold N"), cannot pass: line 22 `tapOn: "Contact Seller"` runs from the Bazaar feed but that label exists only on ListingDetail.tsx:900, so no listing is opened; line 25 `tapOn: "Seller"` taps the section heading (ListingDetail.tsx:645-649), not the navigating UserIdentity testID "seller-profile-link" (ListingDetail.tsx:663); line 29 `assertVisible: "Seller Profile"` never appears loaded (UserProfile.tsx:350 renders title={profile.name}; that string is only in the :249/:276 loading/error headers). FIX: `tapOn: {index: 0, testID: "listing-card"}` then `tapOn: {testID: "seller-profile-link"}`, drop the "Seller Profile" assert ("Active Listings"/"Joined" are the stable anchors), and add a testID to TransactionStatsBadge so `assertVisible: "1 sold"` stops matching localized copy. | MED hatiwal-api/app/models/user.rb#recompute_transaction_counters! (~L59-82) + app/models/transaction.rb:41-51 - ListingDashboard::FORM_ATTRIBUTES permits `:status` (app/dashboards/listing_dashboard.rb:69-78), so an admin flipping a sold Listing back to active re-opens the mobile sold path and creates a SECOND sold Transaction for one listing (2/2 counters for one real sale), which the new recompute cannot repair because it takes max(transactions, sold listings); the comment at transaction.rb:45-47 is false in effect. FIX: `Transaction.as_seller(self).sold.distinct.count(:listing_id)` plus the as_buyer equivalent, and/or refuse a second sold Transaction per listing; correct the comment. | MED hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/shared/user-profile/ProfileHeader.tsx never renders ResponseRateBadge, though the spec puts the new stat "near member-since + response-rate" and maestro/browse/seller_response_rate_badge.yaml:44-51 asserts it on the profile - deleting SellerProfile.tsx removed the last code path, so that flow is permanently unsatisfiable and tapping through from ListingDetail.tsx:667 to the full profile LOSES a trust signal. FIX: render `<ResponseRateBadge responseRatePercent={profile.responseRatePercent} responseTimeLabel={profile.responseTimeLabel} />` beside the TransactionStatsBadge wrapper (ProfileHeader.tsx:141-145) - data already ships in src/api/users.ts:26-31 and it self-suppresses on null/0. | MED visual hierarchy: ProfileHeader.tsx StatCell:39-52 renders "Active Listings" at fontSize 20 / weight 700 / colors.primary while src/components/common/TransactionStatsBadge.tsx:60-72 renders "N sold - N bought" as a 12px colors.mutedForeground line indistinguishable from the Clock recency row. FIX: add `variant?: "meta" | "pill"` to TransactionStatsBadge (do not fork it) - pill = colors.muted bg, rounded-full, paddingHorizontal 10 / paddingVertical 4, text-xs font-semibold, number in colors.foreground, Handshake in colors.primary - and render it in the CENTERED identity block under RatingDisplay (ProfileHeader.tsx:96-102). | LOW hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/shared/Profile.tsx:107-172 - fillerCount is provably always 0 for the only caller (:411 passes columns={2} with 1 or 2 stats), so the `columns` prop, slotCount, fillerCount, the filler Views, the profile-stats-filler testID and 3 of 6 tests are unreachable. FIX: delete them plus the "pads a partially-filled MULTI-stat grid" test; keep the divider behaviour and its test. | LOW hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/shared/UserProfile.tsx:534,547-551 - the RTL chevron flip hit only the hand-rolled HeaderBar back button, so shared src/components/common/BackButton.tsx still renders an unflipped ChevronLeft (as do AllReviews.tsx:129, ListingDetail.tsx:689, MyListingDetail.tsx:259) and ps/fa now has two contradictory back affordances; the hand-rolled Pressable also has no accessibilityLabel (TalkBack says bare "button") and no testID="back_button". FIX: move `const BackIcon = isRtl ? ChevronRight : ChevronLeft` into BackButton and replace HeaderBar's Pressable with `<BackButton onPress={onBack} />` (t("common.goBack")/t("common.back") both exist in en/ps/fa). | LOW ProfileHeader.tsx:113 `marginBottom: activeLabelText ? 8 : 16` predates the conditional badge wrapper at :141-145, so with counts but no lastActiveLabel the badge sits 16px from the stats it annotates and 4px from the unrelated bio card. FIX: `const hasMetaRow = !!activeLabelText || !!profile.soldCount || !!profile.boughtCount;` use `marginBottom: hasMetaRow ? 8 : 16`, and give whichever meta row is LAST the trailing marginBottom: 16. | LOW typography: ProfileHeader.tsx uses className="text-xs" at :160 but StatCell hardcodes fontSize 20 (:42), 12 (compact) and 11 (label :55, below the documented text-xs=12 meta floor), plus 14 (bio :193) and 15 (section label :218); UserProfile.tsx hardcodes 14 (:508) and 16 (:554, :291), while ProfileStatsGrid on the own profile uses text-xl/text-xs. FIX: convert StatCell to className="text-xl font-bold" / "text-xs" (keep colours in style). | LOW hatiwal-mobile/src/components/common/TransactionStatsBadge.tsx:70 - the label Text has no flexShrink (RN default 0) in a flex row, so the ~2x longer ps/fa labels clip. FIX: `style={{ color: colors.mutedForeground, flexShrink: 1 }}`; also set accessible + accessibilityLabel on the wrapping View from the two localized parts joined by ", " so readers stop voicing the raw " - " separator. | LOW hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/shared/UserProfile.tsx:396-406 - use ListingFeed's emptyIllustration (src/components/common/ListingFeed.tsx:99-103; ListingsIllustration, as Browse.tsx/MyListings.tsx do) rather than a bare emptyIcon, and replace the action label t("profile.quickActions.myListings") - "My Listings" is a destination, not the primary ACTION the design system wants - with the existing post/create-listing key so the CTA is a verb in all 3 locales. | LOW hatiwal-api/lib/tasks/transactions.rake has no spec, is undocumented outside its own comment so no operator knows the recovery lever exists, and runs 3 queries per user in a find_each loop. FIX: add a rake spec (`Rake::Task["transactions:recompute_counters"].invoke` against drifted rows), mention it in the backend docs, and consider two grouped `UPDATE ... FROM (SELECT ... GROUP BY)` statements. | LOW hatiwal-mobile/docs/TESTING_AND_NAVIGATION.md:122 still maps row 14 "Seller profile" to the deleted src/screens/shared/SellerProfile.tsx in the table agents use to find a screen. ## TASK-M263
- **Title**: Meetup proposals: pick an exact location on the map (real lat/long pin, not fuzzy text)
- **Type**: frontend
- **Priority**: P2
- **Status**: DONE
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: Approved and shipped (2026-07-05). Built by house-2: exact-location meetup pins reusing LocationRangePicker point mode, backward-compatible 3-part message body, RTL/dark/3-locale clean. Code review APPROVED. Design review's one must-fix — the "Pick on map" control and its clear-✕ were below the 44pt touch target — is now fixed: clear-✕ given an explicit minWidth/minHeight: 44 hit box (it previously had only a 13px icon + hitSlop, no box of its own), and the outer "Pick on map" Pressable's hitSlop bumped 8→12. tsc clean; full Jest suite 1200/1200 passing. Committed as hatiwal-mobile `f9a2c39`.
- **Description**: The in-chat meetup scheduler is currently text-only: MeetupSheet sends body = 'place | time' and MessageBubble's openInMaps does a fuzzy geo:0,0?q=<text> query — tapping the location does NOT drop a precise pin, defeating safe-meetup coordination. Let the proposer optionally pin an exact spot on a map, reusing the existing LocationRangePicker point mode — no new library, no backend change (coordinates encoded in the existing free-form message body, backward compatible). MOBILE ONLY: MeetupSheet 'Pick on map' button opening LocationRangePicker point mode; backward-compatible body encoding 'place | time | <lat>,<long>' via new pure helper meetupBody.ts (encodeMeetupBody/parseMeetupBody tolerating the 2-part legacy format); MessageBubble parses coords and openInMaps uses REAL coordinates (Android geo:, iOS maps:) with fallback to text query for legacy; pin icon on the meetup bubble when precise location attached; new strings in en/ps/fa; Jest tests for the helper + URL building. Out of scope: any backend/message-schema change, live location sharing, changing the offer/accept/decline flow.
- **Acceptance**: When proposing a meetup, the seller/buyer can optionally pin an exact location on the map (reusing LocationRangePicker point mode); the Place field auto-fills with the place name and lat/long is captured. Sending produces a backward-compatible body; a legacy 2-part meetup still renders and still opens a text search. When coordinates are present, the meetup bubble shows a pin icon and tapping 'Open in Maps' drops the precise pin (Android geo: / iOS maps: with real ll). encodeMeetupBody/parseMeetupBody round-trip both formats and tolerate malformed input (Jest green). No new library, no API change; works in ps/fa RTL + dark, all 3 locales present, no hardcoded colors/strings, no console errors.

## TASK-Y316
- **Title**: "Price dropped" badge on Saved screen - lure buyers back when a saved listing's price falls
- **Type**: fullstack
- **Priority**: P1
- **Status**: DONE
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: Approved. Addressed all reviewer-flagged issues from the "Price dropped" badge (Saved screen) review round. Backend was already PASS (no changes needed — SavedListing#price_at_save/price_dropped?/price_drop_amount + serializer already correct, single-pass, RSpec/Rubocop clean per the review). Mobile fixes:

1. MEDIUM — Double price-drop signal: ListingCard.tsx now suppresses the listing-wide priceDropPercent corner overlay (grid) / inline pill (list) whenever the per-buyer priceDropped saved badge is already showing on the same card, so only one green "price dropped" cue ever renders per card (`!listing.priceDropped` guard added to both the grid overlay and list-row conditions).

2. MEDIUM — Accessibility hid actual prices: PriceDropBadge's 'saved' variant no longer sets a generic accessibilityLabel; it now builds the label from the real interpolated values via two new i18n keys (`listing.priceDrop.savedBadgeA11y` for the full form, `savedBadgeCompactA11y` for the compact form), added to en/ps/fa, so a screen reader announces the old and new price, not just "Price dropped".

3. LOW — Redundant current price + content-heavy grid pill: added a `compact` prop to PriceDropBadge's 'saved' variant. Compact form (used by ListingCard's grid variant, which sits directly under the PriceTag hero) drops the "Price dropped" label text and the duplicated current-price figure, rendering just the struck-through old price + a bold drop amount (e.g. "AFN 100000 -AFN 15000") on one line — no more two-line wrap on narrow 2-column cards. The list-row variant (which has more horizontal room) keeps the original full old→new form unchanged.

4. NIT (hardcoded font sizes) — left as-is per the review's own note that it's non-blocking and consistent with the rest of the file.

Verified with Jest inside the `hatiwal-mobile-mobile-1` container: both PriceDropBadge.test.tsx and ListingCard.test.tsx pass (84/84), including new tests for the compact form, the a11y label content, and the double-badge suppression on both grid and list variants. Also confirmed `tsc --noEmit` produces zero errors referencing PriceDropBadge.tsx or ListingCard.tsx (the only tsc errors present are pre-existing, unrelated ones in SellerListingCard.test.tsx). Storybook stories extended with compact/RTL/dark states for the new prop.
- **Description**: A buyer who saved an item has no way to know its price later dropped - a high-intent conversion moment currently missed. Show a 'Price dropped' badge on the Saved screen comparing the current price to the price at the moment the buyer saved it. Distinct from TASK-N804 (generic price-history badge on listing detail/card, based on listing's own history) and TASK-N612 (saved-search new-match count) - this is per-buyer, per-save price-delta on the Saved tab. BACKEND (hatiwal-api): Migration adds price_at_save (decimal, matching listings.price type) to saved_listings; backfill existing rows with listing's current price; set in app/models/saved_listing.rb via before_create copying listing.price. Extend the saved-listings serializer used by GET /my/saved_listings (endpoint from TASK-S063) to expose price_at_save, price_dropped boolean (listing.price < price_at_save && listing.active?), and price_drop_amount; keep it single-pass (no N+1 - the listings are already joined). Pundit unchanged (owner-scoped); add/extend RSpec request + serializer specs for dropped/unchanged/increased. rspec + rubocop clean. MOBILE (hatiwal-mobile): Saved screen src/screens/buyer/Saved.tsx: when a card's priceDropped is true, render a compact 'Price dropped' badge with old price struck through and new price emphasized. Implement as a small shared PriceDropBadge component under src/components/common/ (compose existing PriceTag/StatusBadge tokens - do NOT fork PriceTag) for reuse. useLocalization() for currency; useColors() for success/attention tone. en/ps/fa label; RTL correct (strikethrough + arrow mirror); dark correct. Tests: Jest + Storybook for PriceDropBadge (.stories.tsx + __tests__) covering dropped/no-drop/RTL; backend RSpec as above.
- **Acceptance**: After a buyer saves a listing, if the seller later lowers the price, the Saved tab shows a 'Price dropped' badge on that card with the original price struck through and the new lower price emphasized (localized currency); no badge when unchanged/higher or listing inactive. GET /my/saved_listings returns price_at_save, price_dropped, price_drop_amount with no N+1. Existing saved rows backfilled by the migration. PriceDropBadge is a shared component (not a PriceTag fork) with Jest + Storybook coverage in all states; label localized en/ps/fa; RTL + dark correct; backend rspec + rubocop clean; no console errors.

I did not touch `docs/SPRINT_BOARD.md`'s Status/Session fields — those are owned by a separate board-write step in this repo's pipeline (confirmed by git history: dev commits like a379dac don't touch board status; a distinct "cycle N DONE" commit does that after review), and the top of the file explicitly warns against manually editing IN_PROGRESS tasks.
- **Description**: PROBLEM (verified in code): in `hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/seller/ListingForm.tsx` EVERY exit path blows the stack away and lands on the buyer feed — `saveMutation.onSuccess` (line 360), `publishMutation.onSuccess` (line 386), and `onCancel` (lines 410 and 421) all call `router.replace("/(main)/(tabs)/browse")`. Consequences: (a) a seller who just published is dropped into the BUYER Browse feed with nothing but a toast — no way to see, share or manage the listing they just created, at the single highest-intent moment in the app, and word-of-mouth sharing is the primary growth lever for a marketplace with no payment and no delivery; (b) a seller who edits an already-active listing from My Listings / MyListingDetail is thrown out to Browse and loses their place; (c) cancelling an edit does the same. Note the web client already gets this right (`hatiwal-web/src/components/listing/listing-form.tsx:291` pushes to `/my-listings/{id}`), so this is a mobile-only defect — no web mirror card needed.

BUILD (mobile only; no backend, no new library):
1. `ListingForm.tsx`: change both mutations from `onSuccess: ()` to `onSuccess: (listing)` — `listingsAPI.createListingWithImages`, `updateListingWithImages` and `publishListing` all resolve the listing.
2. CREATE path (draft or publish): `router.replace(`/(main)/my-listings/${listing.id}`)` — the seller lands on `src/screens/seller/MyListingDetail.tsx` (route `app/(main)/my-listings/[id].tsx`) where Publish / Reserve / Edit / analytics already live. Never the Browse tab.
3. New `src/screens/seller/listing-form/PublishSuccessSheet.tsx` on `@gorhom/bottom-sheet`, following the existing `MeetupSheet.tsx` / `OfferSheet.tsx` pattern: cover thumbnail + title + shared `PriceTag`, headline + subtitle, and three actions — 'Share listing' (reuse `resolveShareUrl` and `buildShareBody` from `src/utils/shareUtils.ts` with RN `Share.share`, exactly as ListingDetail does — do NOT write new share code), 'View as buyer' → `/(main)/listing/[id]`, 'Post another' → `router.replace("/(main)/listing/new")`. Present it over MyListingDetail by passing a `published=1` route param from the publish success, and clear the param immediately after showing so it can never reappear on focus or back-navigation. Shown only after publishing a NEW listing.
4. EDIT path: on save of an existing listing, `router.canGoBack() ? router.back() : router.replace(`/(main)/my-listings/${listing.id}`)`. Same for `onCancel`, after the existing `confirmAlert` discard confirmation. Keep the existing `invalidateListingCaches()` calls so the origin screen shows fresh data.
5. i18n: add `listing.form.publishSuccess.{title,subtitle,share,viewAsBuyer,postAnother,done}` to `src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/listing.json`, fully written in real Pashto and Dari.
6. Tests: `PublishSuccessSheet.stories.tsx` (light/dark + RTL) and a Jest test asserting the three actions render from the i18n keys and that Share is invoked with the resolved share URL; Maestro `maestro/seller/publish_success.yaml` covering publish → sheet → 'View as buyer', and edit-save → returns to the origin screen.
- **Acceptance**: Publishing a new listing lands the seller on their own listing at `/(main)/my-listings/:id` — never the Browse tab — with a success sheet offering Share listing / View as buyer / Post another; dismissing leaves them on the owner detail. Saving a NEW listing as a draft also lands on the owner detail (draft state, Publish action visible), not Browse. Saving an edit of an existing listing returns to the previous screen (My Listings or the owner detail) with refreshed data, and cancelling after the discard confirm does the same — neither replaces the stack with Browse. The success sheet appears exactly once per publish (no reappearance on focus or back). Share goes through the existing `shareUtils` helpers with no new share code. All colors via `useColors()`, every string via `t()` in all 3 locales, RTL correct in `ps` and `fa`, dark mode correct, no raw `Alert.alert` (only `confirmAlert`), Storybook story + Jest test + Maestro flow added, Jest suite green, `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, no console errors.

## TASK-WEB-B2BAR

- **ReviewNotes**: Approved. CHANGES-REQUESTED pass on the sticky listing action bar. The ENOTIMP-aborted cycle 16 had already landed most of the code fixes on disk; I verified each against the ReviewNotes rather than rebuilding, then closed the three things that were genuinely still open (all of them test/doc, plus one lint leftover). | VERIFIED ALREADY FIXED (read the code, not the prose): • BLOCKING contrast + BLOCKING reduced-motion — src/lib/unsettled.ts no longer owns any class names: the `tone` argument is gone, `unsettledProps` returns ARIA only, and each caller swaps its own mark on a held tap (Heart/MessageCircle/Flag → Loader2). No `animate-pulse`, no `opacity-70`, so the 1.90:1 trough and the 3.38:1 pulsed label are gone and a reduced-motion viewer has a cue. The docblock's inherited "~3.1:1" is corrected to ≈2.0:1 in both places. • MEDIUM held-tap release — save-button.tsx now feeds `identityUnresolved` into `useQueuedTap`'s `pending` in its own right, so the presentational budget cannot release a tap into `status === "loading"`. On a hint-less ISR page the budget still releases (guest and signed-in are provably indistinguishable there — `hatiwal_viewer_id` is httpOnly), but the fallback is now recoverable. • SHOULD-FIX `?next=` — one `useLoginHref()` helper (src/components/auth/login-href.ts) is used by all gated sites: save-button ×2, start-conversation-button ×2 (push + rendered guest href), report-button ×2, seller-phone-reveal, require-auth. • LOW probe budget — PROBE_BUDGET_MS is derived from PROBE_BACKOFF_MS (12s) instead of a flat 8s. • LOW/NIT element-type stability, save-button docblock drift, `[&_svg]:size-5` on the bar heart — all done. | WHAT I FIXED: 1. e2e/listing-action-bar.spec.ts — cycle 16 shipped `?next=` but left two guest assertions demanding a bare `/login` (`toContain('href="/en/login"')`, `toHaveAttribute("href","/en/login")`). They were never re-run (the review only re-ran the 4 new specs) and fail on today's code. Corrected to pin the recovering href `/en/login?next=%2Flistings%2F2` in the server HTML, and the click-through now asserts `next` survives. 2. e2e/saved.spec.ts — added the missing spec the review asked for: signed-in buyer, ISR page, probe held. Pins that the tap is HELD while an answer is still possible (no save request, no navigation) and that the last-resort fallback is `/en/login?next=%2F`, not a bare `/login`. Also corrected the stale "8s budget" comment (now derived, 12s) and its margin. 3. e2e/listing-action-bar.spec.ts — new spec "a held tap says so by swapping the glyph — with animations off": `emulateMedia({reducedMotion:"reduce"})`, asserts the svg's own markup changes on a held tap and that `opacity` stays 1, so the cue can never regress to an opacity or an animation. 4. start-conversation-button.tsx — removed the now-unused `cn` import (the only eslint warning in the repo, left by the aborted cycle). 5. Docs — MOBILE_TO_WEB_MIGRATION.md B2BAR row gets a cycle-17 note (the glyph-only cue contract, the loginHref centralisation, the derived budget, the new specs); WEB_BACKLOG.md's FlowApp #248 entry now also records the 40px Report trigger the review flagged, so that NIT is decided with the reason rows instead of one control at a time. | Nothing in listing-action-bar.tsx or page.tsx was touched, so the card's acceptance criteria are untouched; no i18n keys added (contactSeller/actionBarLabel present in en/ps/fa), no API or /api/me allow-list change, no react-native/expo import. | VERIFICATION: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; `npx eslint src/ e2e/` clean (0 warnings now); production build clean. E2E, serially: listing-action-bar 24/24, saved 5/5, report + message-seller 11/11, listing-detail 55/57.
- **Description**: PROBLEM (verified in code): on `hatiwal-web/src/app/[locale]/listings/[id]/page.tsx` the buyer CTA block (lines 241-268) sits in the document flow BELOW the gallery, the badge row, the price, the location card (which embeds a 192px-tall Leaflet map) and the seller trust card. On a phone browser — the dominant traffic shape — 'Message seller' is two to three screens down and the price has long scrolled away. The mobile app pins a sticky Message-seller CTA (see the TASK-M617 notes referencing 'the sticky Message-seller CTA'); web has NO sticky element at all except the site header (`grep -rn sticky src` returns only `site-header.tsx:14` and a `lg:sticky` filter sidebar). Buyers scroll, lose the price anchor, and bounce. BUILD (web only — mobile already has this): 1. New client island `hatiwal-web/src/components/listing/listing-action-bar.tsx`. Props: `listingId`, `sellerId`, `status`, `price`, `currency`, `negotiable`, `initialSaved`, `sentinelId`. Layout: `fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-40 lg:hidden border-t bg-background/95 backdrop-blur` with `pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom)]`; leading side = compact `PriceTag` (`@/components/shared/price-tag`, size sm); trailing side = the EXISTING `StartConversationButton` (`@/components/chat/start-conversation-button`) plus `SaveButton` (`@/components/shared/save-button`). Reuse those components verbatim — do NOT re-implement the start-conversation / offer / save mutations (rule 1: no duplication). 2. Visibility: `useEffect` + `IntersectionObserver` on `document.getElementById(sentinelId)`; the bar is hidden while the inline CTA block is on screen and slides in (`translate-y` + `transition-transform`) once it scrolls out. Render `null` when `useAuth()` resolves the viewer as the seller (`user.id === sellerId`), or when `status !== "active"` (sold/reserved already show a notice inline). 3. Edit `page.tsx`: give the existing actions wrapper `id="listing-actions"`, render `<ListingActionBar … sentinelId="listing-actions" />` at the end of the page, and add `pb-28 lg:pb-6` to the page container so the bar never covers the report link or the site footer. 4. API: none new — the bar reuses `POST /listings/:id/conversations`, `POST /conversations/:id/messages` and `POST|DELETE /listings/:id/save` through the reused components. i18n: reuse the existing `listing.*` / `common.*` keys; if an aria-label is needed add `listing.detail.actionBarLabel` to `messages/en.json`, `ps.json` and `fa.json` (real Pashto/Dari).
- **Acceptance**: On a viewport ≤1023px, scrolling past the inline 'Message seller' CTA reveals a fixed bottom bar showing the listing price plus the Message-seller and Save controls; scrolling back up hides it again; no bar at `lg` and above. The bar is absent for the listing's own seller and for `reserved`/`sold` listings. Tapping Message-seller in the bar opens the identical dialog and resolves/creates the SAME conversation as the inline button (StartConversationButton is reused, not copied), and toggling save in either place leaves both hearts in the same state. The bar honours `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)`, never overlaps the report link or footer (page has extra bottom padding), mirrors correctly in `ps`/`fa` RTL (price on the trailing side), and is correct in light and dark. `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` both clean.

No rebuild was needed — the cycle-4 implementation in commit 56ec7b0 is complete, correct, and green, and I found no defect worth a code change. BOTH ORIGINAL DEFECTS CLOSED: (a) the category rail is headed t("listing.detail.similarListings") — translated in en/ps/fa; t("home.recent") now survives in exactly one correct place, the home feed (src/app/[locale]/page.tsx:95); no locale key is left unused (the key is listing.detail.similarListings, not listing.similarListings as the ticket text said). (b) The "More from this Seller" rail exists, keyed listing.detail.moreFromSeller, in all 3 catalogs. ALL SIX CYCLE-3 DESIGN FINDINGS CLOSED IN CODE: viewAllHref on the Similar rail (→ /categories/{slug}, gated on isActive so it never duplicates UnavailableActions' CTA); loading state (CrossSellRailsSkeleton behind <Suspense> at page.tsx:359, same 2→4 rail tracks); over-fetch (seller fetch is pageSize RAIL_SIZE+1 = 5, similar comes from the server-capped GET /listings/:id/similar); rail boundary (Separator, only when bothRails, placed INSIDE HideForOwner so it cannot dangle); listing-card.tsx hardcoded sizes (now CARD_IMAGE_SIZES[tracks], typed by the same key as the grid's GRID_COLUMNS so a missing sizes hint is a type error); accessible names (each rail is a named region via aria-label, and "View all" appends the section title sr-only AFTER the visible words per WCAG 2.5.3). Cap/dedupe audited: RAIL_SIZE+1 covers the only case that needs it (source listing coming back in the seller's page); the seller rail wins the Set dedupe so no card appears twice; a sold/reserved source is never in a status:active page so the filter is a harmless no-op. VERIFY RUNS — tsc --noEmit clean; npm run build succeeded (lint + RSC + prerender, all locales); eslint on all task files 0 problems; Playwright e2e/listing-detail.spec.ts 36/36 PASSED on isolated ports incl. all 18 rail specs (contents, source excluded, seller-above-similar ordering, dedupe, divider present/never dangling, named regions, ps locale prefix, owner-hidden rail, single seller CTA on sold/reserved, and "labelled 'Similar Listings', not 'Recent'"); backend spec/models/listing_spec.rb .similar_to 4/4, spec/requests/api/v1/listings_spec.rb:841+:951 9/9, rubocop 0 offenses on the 3 touched API files. INTENTIONAL MOBILE DIVERGENCE (not a defect): mobile shows Similar first then seller, cap 8, no dedupe (ListingDetail.tsx:715/751); web shows seller first, cap 4, deduped — exactly what the acceptance criteria specify and what the seller-first dedupe rule requires. Documented in the migration row. FLAGS FOR THE REVIEWERS: (1) Concurrent sessions are editing the tree live — git status went from clean to 16 modified files mid-pass, including a large in-flight ViewerIdProvider/SSR-owner refactor of page.tsx + owner-gate.tsx and conversations_controller.rb in the API; none of it is mine, do not attribute it to M547. My e2e run executed against that tree and still passed 36/36, so the rails survive the refactor. (2) npm run lint is broken repo-wide: eslint ignores .next but not .next-*, so 9 leftover gitignored scratch build dirs produce all 243 reported errors — zero come from source. (3) Two committed scratch files violate the AGENTS.md clean-tree rule: pw.local.searchhist.config.ts (its own docstring says "TEMPORARY local config (deleted after the run)") and ss.json (a raw saved-search API dump); left in place because a live session is using that config. (4) Known documented gap: rails are fetched anonymously so a signed-in buyer's "Not interested" listings can still surface — the browse feed and category hubs share it; the fix belongs in the fetch layer once for all of them. BUILD ENV NOTE: npm run build first failed with EACCES unlinking .next/server/chunks/ssr (root-owned from an earlier Docker run). Worked around by moving .next aside to .next-root-owned-orphan-m547 (gitignored; the root-owned files cannot be deleted without sudo) and building fresh — same workaround earlier sessions used. My own scratch dist dir .next-e2e-3220 was deleted and its tsconfig.json include entry reverted; the two include entries still in the diff belong to the concurrent suites (.next-e2e and the live :3230 run).
- **Description**: TWO VERIFIED DEFECTS in `hatiwal-web/src/app/[locale]/listings/[id]/page.tsx`:
(a) The cross-sell rail at line 303-308 is a category-similarity query (`getListings({ categoryId, status: 'active' })`) but is rendered under the heading `t("home.recent")` = 'Recent listings' — factually wrong copy. The correct key `listing.similarListings` ('Similar Listings' / 'ورته توکي' / 'آگهی‌های مشابه') already exists at line 163 of `messages/en.json`, `ps.json` and `fa.json` and is currently referenced NOWHERE in `src` (dead string).
(b) There is no 'More from this seller' rail. Mobile ships one (`hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/shared/ListingDetail.tsx` lines 254-769, key `listing.detail.moreFromSeller`, TASK-M547 DONE). A buyer who has just been convinced by the seller trust card (verified badge, rating, response rate, last-active) has no way to see that seller's other stock without abandoning the page.

BUILD:
1. Swap the similar-rail heading from `t("home.recent")` to `t("listing.similarListings")`.
2. Add one server-side fetch beside the existing similar fetch, skipped entirely when `listing.seller` is absent: `safe(getListings({ userId: listing.seller.id, status: "active", pageSize: 12 }), EMPTY_LISTINGS)` — `userId` is already mapped to `user_id` in `ListingsQuery`/`toParams` in `hatiwal-web/src/lib/api/listings.ts`, hitting the existing `GET /api/v1/listings?user_id=&status=active` (the same endpoint mobile uses). Filter out `listing.id`, then `.slice(0, 4)`.
3. Render it as a section ABOVE the Similar-listings section using the shared `ListingGrid` (`@/components/shared/listing-grid` — do not fork), heading `t("listing.detail.moreFromSeller")` with a trailing 'View all' link to `/sellers/${listing.seller.id}` (reuse an existing `viewAll` key). Both rails are public info, so no owner gating.
4. i18n: add `listing.detail.moreFromSeller` ('More from this Seller') to `messages/en.json`, `ps.json`, `fa.json`, mirroring the mobile wording in `hatiwal-mobile/src/i18n/locales/*/listing.json`.
- **Acceptance**: The category rail is now headed 'Similar Listings' (localized in en/ps/fa) instead of 'Recent listings', and no locale key is left unused. When the seller has other active listings, a 'More from this Seller' grid of up to 4 shared `ListingCard`s renders above Similar listings, excludes the listing being viewed, links each card to its own detail page, and offers a 'View all' link to `/sellers/[id]`. The whole section (heading included) is absent when the seller has no other active listing and when `listing.seller` is missing — no empty heading, no empty grid. The rail costs exactly one extra Rails request and a failed fetch degrades to no section via `safe()` rather than a 500. All 3 locales present, RTL correct in `ps`/`fa` (heading and 'View all' on the correct sides), correct in light and dark, no hardcoded strings or hex colors. `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` clean.

## TASK-Z684
- **Title**: Search your conversations on the mobile Conversations screen (name / listing / last message)
- **Type**: frontend
- **Priority**: P2
- **Status**: DONE
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: Approved. Addressed the CHANGES_REQUESTED review notes on TASK-Z684 (Conversations list search). The prior implementation (search bar, filterConversations, SearchBar retrofit on BrowseHeader, translations, tests, maestro flow) was already substantially built and stayed untouched where correct — I fixed every reviewer-flagged defect I could verify and reproduce, added regression tests for each, and left two LOW/design-judgment items for marketplace-designer as noted below. Note: this session's dispatch pointed at hatiwal-mobile/docs/SPRINT_BOARD.md's "## TASK-Z684" section (not the FlowApp Kanban board — no matching card exists there), so no FlowApp board move was performed; SPRINT_BOARD.md itself is owned by the orchestrating fleet script, not edited directly. | HIGH fixes: 1. conversationPreviewText.ts had no `offer_counter` case, so a counter-offer's raw "amount|currency|listedPrice" metadata leaked into both the row display and search matching. Added a shared `case "offer": case "offer_counter":` branch (same parse/format, Tag icon, per-kind translation key) and fixed the previously-dead `chat.preview.offerCounter` key from `"{{amount}} {{currency}}"` to `"{{price}}"` in en/ps/fa to match the formatCurrency-based pattern `offer` already used. 2. Conversations.tsx's read-state (All/Unread/Read) active-chip color used `colors.secondary`/`secondaryForeground`, which are pixel-identical to `colors.muted`/`foreground` in dark mode (useColors.ts) — the selected chip was invisible in dark mode. Recolored to `primaryAlpha` background + `primary` border/text, distinct from the role group's solid-`primary`-fill treatment beside it. 3. UniversalList rendered `EmptyState` (unmounting FlashList) whenever `visibleItems.length === 0`, so `onEndReached` could never fire again — a dead end for (a) Conversations' fetcher-side unread filter when the only unread thread is on an unloaded page (showed a false "All caught up!"), and (b) a search term with no match yet loaded (only escape was "Clear search"). Added an effect that auto-continues fetching remaining pages (bounded by `totalPages`, so it can't loop forever) whenever the visible view is empty but more pages exist, showing a spinner instead of a terminal empty state until pages are exhausted or a match appears. | MEDIUM fixes: 4. filterConversations matched the `formatCurrency`-formatted offer/counter-offer preview, which renders Eastern-Arabic digits in ps/fa (Intl "fa-AF"/"fa-IR") — a Latin-digit search term got zero matches. Both needle and haystack now run through the existing `normalizeDigits` util before comparing. 5. `handleEndReached` computed `nextPage` from React state (`currentPage`), captured by a stale closure; `fetchingMoreRef` was released in `finally` before the state commit landed, leaving a tight window where a re-entrant call could re-request the same page. Replaced with `loadedPageRef`, written synchronously the instant a page is reserved (before the network call starts), and kept in sync across `loadFirst`/`handleRefresh`/`refreshLoadedPages`. 6. `refreshLoadedPages` fired one `Promise.all` over every loaded page; a deep auto-paged list would turn every focus event into a large parallel burst. Now batches in chunks of 5 concurrent requests. 7. Filter/role chips had `minHeight: 44` on the same node as the visible pill (background/radius), so the pill itself grew to a near-square 44pt lozenge instead of the intended compact chip. Restructured to a transparent 44pt outer `Pressable` wrapping a compact inner pill View (paddingVertical 7, fontSize 13) — 44pt tap target preserved, visual chip stays compact. 8. SearchBar's clear button's `entering`/`exiting` FadeIn/FadeOut fought AnimatedPressable's own `useAnimatedStyle` opacity (both targeting the same native prop on the same node) — the fade likely didn't play. AnimatedPressable now skips writing `opacity` from its press-feedback style whenever `entering`/`exiting` are supplied (scale-only feedback in that case); entering/exiting exclusively own opacity. 9. SearchBar's clear-button fade was unconditional; every other entering/exiting animation in the codebase gates on `useReduceMotion()`. Added the same gate. 10. Filter chips, role chips, and the Inbox/Archived tab toggle had no `accessibilityRole`/`accessibilityState`/`accessibilityLabel` — added to all three, particularly important now that dark-mode selection was previously communicated by color alone. | LOW fixes: 11. AnimatedPressable's `entering`/`exiting` were typed `any` with a comment claiming Reanimated doesn't export the builder type — false for the installed 4.1.7 (`EntryOrExitLayoutType` is exported). Fixed the types and removed the incorrect comment. 12. SearchBar's clear button used `margin: -14` on all sides, overlapping ~14pt into the row gap and ~6pt into the Input's tail (tapping just past typed text hit Clear instead of placing the cursor). Changed to `marginVertical: -14, marginHorizontal: -6`. 13. Conversations.tsx composed two translated strings in code with a hardcoded ASCII space (`noMatchDescription` + `partialResultsConversations`) for the "search matched nothing loaded, more pages exist" copy. Added one new `chat.search.noMatchDescriptionPartial` key (full sentence) in en/ps/fa; code now calls `t()` once. 14. Chip label fontSize was 12 vs the house's 13 (CategoryChipRow/ConditionChips), and the screen title was 22 vs the `text-2xl`/24 scale in DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §3 — both bumped. | Explicitly deferred to marketplace-designer (LOW, design-judgment, out of feature-builder scope per the house workflow): - Search-result term highlighting in ConversationRow's preview line (no visual cue for *why* a row matched). - Showing a price (`PriceTag`) on the conversation row — "north star" price-prominence polish. | Noted but intentionally NOT touched: this task's board comment flagged that MessageBubble.tsx/Conversation.tsx/reserveAfterAccept.ts/BuyerPickerSheet.tsx carry unrelated riding changes not reviewed as part of TASK-Z684. I left those files alone (only touched conversationPreviewText.ts, which IS in scope for the offer_counter fix). Running the full Jest suite surfaces 2 pre-existing, unrelated failing suites in exactly those excluded files (offerInThread.test.tsx, MessageBubble.test.tsx) — traced to `OutcomeBadge` referencing `CheckCircle2`/`XCircle` icons that aren't in those test files' `lucide-react-native` mocks, plus an `accessibilityState` shape assertion mismatch. Confirmed via full-repo diff of failures before/after my changes that this is unrelated to anything I touched (same 2 suites fail on a clean run with none of my edits applied to those files). Flagging for a separate fix/reviewer, not fixed here to avoid scope creep into files this task's reviewer explicitly said should not ride along. | Testing: `npx jest` — 1794/1797 tests pass across 107/109 suites (the 2 failing suites are the pre-existing, out-of-scope ones above). All touched-file suites are 100% green: conversationPreviewText.test.ts, filterConversations.test.ts, ConversationRow.test.tsx (44 tests), UniversalList.test.tsx (35 tests, +8 new), SearchBar.test.tsx (18 tests, +3 new), CategoryChipRow.test.tsx. `npx tsc --noEmit` shows zero new errors in any touched source file (only the same pre-existing repo-wide @types/jest/@storybook config gaps in test files, unchanged from before this session).
- **Description**: PROBLEM (verified in code): src/screens/chat/Conversations.tsx offers an inbox/archived tab pair and an all/unread/read chip filter, but there is NO way to search. Once a user has a dozen threads the only way to find 'the buyer about the iPhone' is to scroll. Chat is where every Hatiwal deal happens, so this is a core findability gap. (Mobile already ships in-thread message search — TASK-N803 — so this is the missing list-level half.)

BUILD (mobile only; no API change):
1. New shared src/components/common/SearchBar.tsx — RTL-aware muted container + lucide Search icon + RNR `Input` (src/components/reusables/input) + optional clear X + optional debounceMs, colors via useColors() inline styles (never className for colors), plus SearchBar.stories.tsx covering empty / typed / clear / RTL / dark. This is deliberately the `<SearchBar>` half of open refactor ticket R15 in docs/REFACTOR_DUPLICATION.md so this feature does not hand-roll a third copy of the search row; R15's FilterChip/ChipScrollRow/CategoryChips work stays untouched.
2. Retrofit the existing browse search row (src/screens/buyer/browse/BrowseHeader.tsx, the muted container + Search icon + Input block) to use <SearchBar> with its current 400ms server debounce and animated clear button — rendered output must stay visually identical.
3. Add <SearchBar> above the tab/filter rows in Conversations.tsx (instant client-side, clear X). Extract the predicate into src/screens/chat/conversations/filterConversations.ts: case-insensitive, trimmed match against otherParticipant?.name (falling back to buyer?.name / seller?.name), listing?.title, and lastMessageBody. Feed the term into the existing makeFetcher(tabMode, filter, term) client-side filter and include it in listConfig.id (debounced ~250ms) so UniversalList re-renders without a skeleton flash. It must compose with — not replace — the archived tab and the all/unread/read filter, and must not affect the unread badge (badge stays derived from the unfiltered allConversationsRef).
4. No-match state via UniversalList's existing empty props: new `chat.search.*` keys (placeholder, noMatchTitle with the term, noMatchDescription, clearSearch) added to all 3 locales (en, ps, fa), with a Clear-search emptyAction.

WEB MIRROR (follow-up card, not this one): the same client-side filter over getConversations() in hatiwal-web/src/components/chat/conversations-view.tsx, which has the identical gap.

TESTS (mandatory per CLAUDE.md): Jest unit for filterConversations (name / title / last-message hits, case-insensitivity, whitespace-only term = no filtering, null listing and null lastMessageBody safe) in src/screens/chat/conversations/__tests__/; Storybook stories for SearchBar; Maestro flow maestro/chat/conversations-search.yaml covering happy path (type → list narrows), no-match (empty state + Clear search restores the full list), and search combined with the unread filter.
- **Acceptance**: 1. Typing on the Conversations screen narrows the list live to threads whose counterpart name, listing title, or last-message preview contains the term (case-insensitive, trimmed); clearing it restores the full list with no skeleton flash. 2. Search composes with the existing controls: it filters within the Archived tab, and search + 'Unread' shows only unread matches. 3. A term with no matches shows the localized no-match empty state naming the term plus a Clear-search action that empties the field. 4. The header/tab unread badge and the chat-store unread total are unchanged by searching (still computed from the unfiltered inbox). 5. Archive / mark-read / mark-unread / delete still work on a filtered row and keep their optimistic behaviour + rollback. 6. src/components/common/SearchBar.tsx exists and is used by BOTH Conversations.tsx and BrowseHeader.tsx; BrowseHeader keeps its 400ms debounce and animated clear and looks unchanged; grep shows no remaining hand-rolled search-input block in either screen. 7. All new strings exist in en, ps and fa — no hardcoded strings; no hardcoded colors (useColors() inline styles only, no className for color). 8. RTL verified on ps and fa: icon, input text alignment and clear X mirror correctly. 9. Light and dark both correct. 10. Jest suite (filter predicate) passes, SearchBar stories render in all states, and maestro/chat/conversations-search.yaml passes.

## TASK-WEB-C2-ACTIONS
- **Title**: Inline lifecycle quick-actions on the web My Listings cards (no more round trip to /my-listings/[id])
- **Type**: web
- **Priority**: P1

- **Session**: -

- **ReviewNotes**: Approved. CHANGES_REQUESTED fix cycle. The feature itself (inline lifecycle quick-actions on /my-listings cards, shared brain in account/listing-actions.tsx, footer/footerFor extension of the shared card+grid) was already built and I VERIFIED it rather than rebuilding — the reviewer's "VERIFIED GOOD" block holds and grep confirms LIFECYCLE/actionsFor exist in exactly one file, imported by manage-listing-view, seller-listing-actions, owner-listing-bar and conversation-thread. I addressed all three outstanding notes: | 1. REQUIRED — scope leak (buyer-facing "No photo" caption). The caption was gated on `showStatus`, which is not an ownership switch: seller/seller-listings-tabs.tsx:71 also passes it on the PUBLIC seller profile's Sold grid, so a buyer on /sellers/[id] → Sold saw "No photo" on every photoless sold card. Added an explicit `nameMissingPhoto?: boolean` to ListingCard + ListingGrid, set ONLY by account/seller-listings-view.tsx (the one owner surface); `showStatus` goes back to meaning "lifecycle badges" everywhere. Then made the leaking surface testable: e2e/mock-api/server.mjs had no `sold_listings` route (the Sold tab silently 404'd into its error state), so I added `GET /users/:id/sold_listings` (Rails :list envelope, filtered by seller + status) — seller 1's "Leather Sofa (Sold)" now renders there — and extended the existing photoless spec to assert the caption is absent on /en/sellers/1 → Sold while the "Sold" badge still renders (so the assertion can't pass on an empty grid). MUTATION-VERIFIED: restoring `showStatus` as the gate makes exactly that new assertion fail (line 316, count 1 vs 0) while the pre-existing /bazaar assertion still passes — i.e. the old spec genuinely could not catch this. | 2. NIT — typography: remote-image.tsx `text-[10px]` → `text-xs` (bottom of the house scale; mobile's two equivalents are 11px). Measured in a real render at 375px 2-column: computed 12px, caption 59px (en) / 58px (ps) / 62px (fa) with scrollWidth === clientWidth, so `truncate` never fires. | 3. NIT — parity claim: the caption suppression on buyer surfaces is now marked as a DELIBERATE DIVERGENCE from mobile (mobile's shared ListingCard captions photoless listings unconditionally, so its buyer feed is captioned too), worded like the kebab's existing "do not 'restore parity'" note, in both listing-card.tsx and the MOBILE_TO_WEB_MIGRATION C2-* row. | VERIFICATION: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; `NODE_ENV=production next build` clean (79 pages); Playwright e2e/my-listings.spec.ts + e2e/seller.spec.ts = 39/39 passed (34 runtime my-listings specs, unchanged block count 29). No new i18n keys (listing.noPhoto already in en/ps/fa), no API/contract change, tokens only, no left/right. Tree left clean (scratch .next dirs and port-scoped auth states removed; tsconfig.json untouched).
- **Description**: PROBLEM (verified in code): on web, /my-listings (src/components/account/seller-listings-view.tsx) renders the generic <ListingGrid hrefFor={l => `/my-listings/${l.id}`}> with no per-card actions. A seller who wants to publish a draft, mark an item sold, reserve it, renew an expired listing, unpublish, or delete must open /my-listings/[id], act, then navigate back — once per listing. Mobile already does all of this inline (src/screens/seller/my-listings/SellerListingCard.tsx owns a renewListing mutation + confirmAlert). Sellers are the scarce side of this marketplace; this is the roughest friction left in the web dashboard.

BUILD (web only; mobile already has it):
1. Extract the shared lifecycle brain out of src/components/account/manage-listing-view.tsx into a new src/components/account/listing-actions.tsx: move the existing `LIFECYCLE` label/success/confirm-key map, `actionsFor(status, expired)` (draft→publish; reserved→sold + [activate]; active+expired→renew + [sold]; active→sold + [reserve, unpublish, renew]; sold→none) and a `useListingLifecycle()` hook wrapping `listingLifecycle`/`deleteMyListing` from src/lib/api/me.ts (toast via the existing `listing.*Success` keys, `qc.invalidateQueries` on ['my-listings'] and ['my-listing', id], busy state, error toast). manage-listing-view.tsx must then IMPORT them — the map and resolver must exist in exactly one file (house rule 1, no duplication).
2. Extend, do NOT fork, the shared card: add an optional `footer?: React.ReactNode` prop to src/components/shared/listing-card.tsx (rendered under the card body in both grid and list variants) and an optional `footerFor?: (listing) => React.ReactNode` to src/components/shared/listing-grid.tsx.
3. New src/components/account/seller-listing-actions.tsx: the footer action row — one primary Button (label from LIFECYCLE via actionsFor) plus a kebab using the existing src/components/ui/dropdown-menu.tsx holding the secondary actions + Edit (link to /listings/[id]/edit) + Delete (destructive). Reuse src/components/ui/confirm-dialog.tsx for every non-buyer action, and reuse the EXISTING src/components/account/sell-buyer-dialog.tsx (SellBuyerDialog) for reserve/sold plus src/components/shared/review-prompt-dialog.tsx when the returned transaction has a buyer — identical behaviour to /my-listings/[id].
4. Wire it in seller-listings-view.tsx via `footerFor`.

ENDPOINTS (no change, all already proxy-allow-listed in src/app/api/me/[...path]/route.ts): PUT /api/me/my/listings/:id/{publish,unpublish,reserve,activate,sold,renew}, DELETE /api/me/my/listings/:id. No new API fields, no serializer change. No new i18n keys expected (all listing.* action/confirm/success keys already exist in messages/{en,ps,fa}.json); if any is added it goes into all 3 locales.
- **Acceptance**: 1. On /my-listings every card shows a primary action matching actionsFor(): draft→Publish, active→Mark sold, active+expired→Renew, reserved→Mark sold, sold→no PRIMARY (amended after two review cycles read this as "no action row": a sold listing keeps its row, because Edit and Delete must still be reachable inline — the kebab simply takes the whole row and carries the "More options" label instead of being a lone glyph); the kebab holds exactly the remaining secondary actions plus Edit and Delete. 2. Mark sold / Reserve open SellBuyerDialog; choosing a buyer records the Transaction and immediately shows ReviewPromptDialog — same as on /my-listings/[id]. 3. Every other action shows the existing confirm dialog (existing listing.confirm* copy) and a success toast (existing listing.*Success copy); Delete is destructive-styled and removes the card. 4. After any action the grid and the per-status tab counts update with no page reload and no skeleton flash (['my-listings'] invalidated); a failed action shows an error toast and leaves the card unchanged. 5. grep shows `actionsFor` and the LIFECYCLE map defined in exactly ONE file, imported by both manage-listing-view.tsx and seller-listing-actions.tsx; listing-card.tsx is extended (new optional prop) and not forked or copied. 6. Buttons/menu are disabled while a mutation is in flight (no double-submit). 7. /ps and /fa: action row and dropdown align to the start/end correctly in RTL; light and dark both readable (tokens only, no hex). 8. `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` are clean.

## TASK-WEB-B3-HISTORY
- **Title**: Recent-searches memory on the web search fields (header + Bazaar sidebar) — mobile parity
- **Type**: web
- **Priority**: P2
- **Status**: DONE
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: Approved. Worked the ReviewNotes fix list on the already-built recent-searches feature (no rebuild — the feature itself was verified, then each note fixed). FIXES 1. MEDIUM correctness (header-search.tsx): the dirty guard no longer drops a published term. A publication that arrives while the field is mid-edit is now stashed in a `skipped` ref and DRAINED on the dirty->clean edge from the debounce effect's no-op branch (`adopt()` also used by the pathname/popstate sync, and cleared in `go()` since `publishBrowseQuery` dedupes by value). I deliberately did NOT take the reviewer's first option (`dirty` in the effect deps): `committed` is a standing value, not an event, so any later return to a clean field re-adopts it — concretely, after searching "sony" and navigating to a listing page, typing and erasing 2 chars in the header would fill the box with the dead term. That counter-scenario is documented in the code comment. 2. LOW test-strictness (e2e/search-history.spec.ts): overflow assertion now `documentElement.scrollWidth - documentElement.clientWidth` (was `window.innerWidth`, which hid ~15px of real overflow behind the scrollbar). 3. LOW docs accuracy: migration row "22 Playwright specs" -> 21 (the file has 21 `test(` blocks; the 23 the previous author saw included the 2 auth.setup fixtures — confirmed, my run reported "Running 23 tests"). 4. LOW duplication: `ui/dropdown-menu.tsx` DropdownMenuContent now imports FLOATING_PANEL_SURFACE (surface-only) instead of re-declaring the recipe; floating-panel.ts docstring updated — nothing is "kept in step" by hand any more. Verified in a real browser that the locale-switcher menu renders identically (radius 10px, 1px border, bg popover, z-50, p-4px, min-w-160px, overflow hidden, 3 items). 5. NIT responsiveness: width floor moved from `lg:` to `md:w-[max(100%,24rem)]`. I measured instead of trusting the note's "~300px tablet field": on /bazaar the header bar field is 379px at 768px (panel 384px, inside the content box, page overflow 0, 6 rows, nothing hidden) and 448px from ~900px, so I corrected the comment + migration note to the measured numbers rather than shipping the estimate. 6. RTL MED: chip label span now has `dir="auto"`. Verified on real renders (/ps 768 + 1280, /en 375/768/900/1280): Latin terms compute `direction: ltr` inside the RTL panel (so they elide at the END and keep the brand word, matching mobile's numberOfLines={1}) while Pashto terms stay `rtl`. 7. Dark-mode contrast: X half hover is `hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-foreground` (was `hover:text-destructive` at ~3:1). Measured at-rest values reproduced the reviewer's exactly (4.29:1 light / 5.78:1 dark) and hover now resolves to the foreground token (~13:1 / ~14.9:1). 8+9. location-search.tsx: suggestion rows get `min-h-11` (were 36px, under the 40px floor) and the OSM attribution `text-[10px]` -> `text-xs`. 10. TEST COVERAGE: the Pashto spec now pins panel/field anchoring — shared RIGHT edge in ps (`|end| <= 1`, start < -1) and the LTR mirror at /en — so a `start-0` -> `left-0` regression fails instead of passing. Observations in the notes (use-browse-query safety, render-phase valueRef write, Clear-all having no undo) needed no action; parity with mobile is correct there. VERIFICATION - `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; eslint clean on every changed file. - E2E: 21/21 search-history tests green. First run: 20 passed / 3 failed, all three the same "debounced search never reached the URL in 45s" timeout under 8-way parallel cold-dev load (2 of them in sidebar-driven code I never touched); re-ran those 3 with --workers=1 -> all pass. - No i18n work needed: all four `browse.*` keys already exist in en/ps/fa with the {term} interpolation. - Left the tree clean: scratch .next dirs removed, my tsconfig.scratch.json entries reverted, tsconfig.json untouched, temp worktree removed. CAVEAT (pre-existing, not from this card): `npm run build` fails at prerender of /404 with "<Html> should not be imported outside of pages/_document". I reproduced it at HEAD (6438929) in a clean detached worktree with a fresh dist dir — identical failure — so acceptance #9's build clause is blocked by a repo-wide break that predates this work and is out of this card's scope. Compile + lint + types all pass. NOT COMMITTED: another session is editing the same tree concurrently (listing-card.tsx, remote-image.tsx, listing-grid.tsx, seller-listings-view.tsx, e2e/my-listings.spec.ts, e2e/mock-api/server.mjs, plus tsconfig.scratch.json churn), so any commit must be path-scoped to the 7 files below. SUGGESTED FOLLOW-UP CARD (from the reviewer's non-blocking observation): an undo toast (sonner is already in the stack) for "Clear all" / chip removal on BOTH clients — mobile's clearHistory behaves the same, so it is a parity-preserving improvement, not a web-only fix.
- **Description**: PROBLEM (verified in code): mobile remembers a buyer's last 10 search terms (src/stores/searchHistory.store.ts, chips in BrowseHeader.tsx with per-term X + Clear all). The web client has NO search memory at all — src/components/layout/header-search.tsx and the Bazaar sidebar search in src/components/browse/browse-client.tsx start empty on every visit. The translations are ALREADY shipped and unused: `browse.recentSearches` and `browse.clearHistory` exist in en/ps/fa while grep for `recentSearches` in src returns nothing. BUILD: 1. New src/lib/use-search-history.ts — localStorage key `hatiwal.searchHistory`, mobile store rules (trim, ignore <2 chars, dedupe, most-recent-first, cap 10), exposing { history, add, remove, clear }; SSR-safe with hydration inside useEffect and try/catch around storage. 2. New src/components/shared/search-history-panel.tsx — heading with a History icon, removable chip per term, clear-all action, tokens only, RTL-aware. 3. Wire BOTH entry points (header-search.tsx and the browse-client sidebar form): add(term) on commit, panel shown only when focused + empty + history non-empty; chip click applies via the existing go()/update({ q }) path; Escape/blur closes. No API change, no new i18n keys, works for guests; never send history to the server or the URL.
- **Acceptance**: 1. Search 'iphone' from the header, then focus the empty header field: 'Recent searches' with an 'iphone' chip appears; the same history appears on the Bazaar sidebar field (one shared store). 2. Reload the page → the chip is still there; clicking it navigates/filters to /bazaar?q=iphone. 3. The X on a chip removes only that term; 'Clear all' empties the list and the panel disappears. 4. The panel never shows while the field has text, and never when history is empty. 5. Cap and hygiene: an 11th search drops the oldest, repeating a term moves it to the front without duplicating, a 1-character term is never stored. 6. Works logged out; nothing search-history-related is sent to Rails and no term is added to the URL beyond the existing ?q=. 7. With localStorage unavailable the page still renders and search still works. 8. /ps and /fa RTL correct; light and dark readable. 9. `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` clean.

Every item on the ReviewNotes fix list is already implemented on disk (web commit 5d34f7c "owner-bar fix-ups (cycle 18)" plus predecessors), and I confirmed each one against the source rather than trusting the commit message: 1) [MED·BLOCKING] reserve/sold action wording in fa+ps — CLOSED. hatiwal-web/messages/fa.json: listing.markReserved = "ثبت رزرو", markSold = "ثبت فروش" (both distinct from status.reserved "رزرو شده" / status.sold "فروخته شده"). messages/ps.json: markReserved = "ریزرو کول", status.reserved = "ریزرو شوی" — the ریزرو loanword family, no longer colliding with the خوندي save/favourite family (common.save "خوندي کول", sidebar.saved "خوندي شوي"). Mirrored byte-for-byte in mobile src/i18n/locales/{fa,ps}/listing.json (AGENTS rule 2 holds). The requested invariant exists as e2e/listing-detail.spec.ts:967 "no locale labels a lifecycle action with its own status word" (per-locale markReserved !== status.reserved, markSold !== status.sold, plus a ps SAVE_ROOT anchor so rewording can't turn it into a no-op). I also swept both clients for stale wording: mobile's remaining "خوندي شوی" hits are all the save sense (browse.saved, listing.unsave, savesCount, form.draftFound) and every reserved-state string in mobile chat/common/listing uses ریزرو — no leftovers, no cross-client drift. 2) [LOW] globals.css --primary-strong — CLOSED, exactly as prescribed: light hsl(221 83% 45%), dark inverted to hsl(217 91% 60%), new --primary-strong-foreground (white / var(--background)) consumed by the ui/badge.tsx `count` variant; e2e/listing-detail.spec.ts:628 now asserts BOTH ≥4.5:1 digits-on-fill and ≥3:1 fill-vs-nearest-opaque-surface, in light and dark. 3) [LOW] auth-nav.tsx unread count invisible to SR — CLOSED: aria-label={unread > 0 ? t("chat.unreadCount", {count}) : t("sidebar.chat")}; chat.unreadCount is present and pluralized in en/ps/fa. 4) [LOW] card.tsx false invariant — CLOSED via the "scope the comment + named ticket" option: the docstring now states the real count (2 importers, ~20 hand-rolled surfaces) and points at R19, which genuinely exists in hatiwal-mobile/docs/REFACTOR_DUPLICATION.md:213 with locations + variants-to-preserve. 5) [LOW] listing-actions.tsx overstated parity — CLOSED: actionsFor's docstring now names the Duplicate gap explicitly (sold is terminal on web, no ?duplicateFrom= flow yet) instead of claiming one-for-one parity. 6) [nit] ~40px dead space at #listing-actions — CLOSED: the whole buyer block is wrapped in <HideForOwner ownerId={listing.seller?.id}> so wrapper + inner div disappear with their contents for the owner (children stay Server Components); covered by spec :810. 7) [nit] Edit spending a band — CLOSED: Edit is demoted into the heading row (HEADING_LINK, ghost, 40px floor) and the action stack is now [next transition] (+ Renew when amber) + Manage + Chats-with-count. Verification run: npx tsc --noEmit clean. Production build passes (79 pages) — IMPORTANT gotcha for the next agent: run it as `NODE_ENV=production NEXT_DIST_DIR=.next-own947 npx next build`; with the shell's inherited NODE_ENV=development the build dies on /404 with the misleading "<Html> should not be imported outside of pages/_document". /[locale]/listings/[id] still builds as ƒ (dynamic), so the cookie-based SSR viewer hint costs no ISR — it was already force-dynamic before this card (verified at 25ac351^). Reviewed the ownership plumbing itself: readViewerIdFromCookies() is httpOnly, never stronger than the token cookies, and is cleared by clearTokenCookies, and useIsOwner is the single shared rule (loading → server hint, else status==="authed" && user.id===ownerId). E2E: e2e/listing-detail.spec.ts = 57/57 green (all 30 owner-panel tests, incl. draft/reserved/sold/expired, ps + fa RTL, dark, 375/412/430px clipping). e2e/listing-action-bar.spec.ts + my-listings.spec.ts + saved.spec.ts = 65/65 green. Mobile jest for the suites touching reserve labels (useListingLifecycle, SellerListingCard, StatusBadge) = 111/111 green. FINDING for whoever runs the suite next (pre-existing, NOT from this card, so I did not patch it): the Playwright suite is flaky at the default 8 workers. Some SSR renders die with "SyntaxError: Unexpected end of JSON input" (a TRUNCATED upstream body — every server-side JSON.parse site already guards an empty one), which reds out whichever specs are unlucky: run 1 killed 7 of this card's owner specs (panel absent because the page render failed), run 2 killed 3 unrelated sold/reserved-recovery specs instead. --workers=1 and a second parallel run were fully green, and hammering the mock API directly at concurrency 24 with keep-alive idle gaps produced 0 bad bodies (720 requests), so the truncation is on the Next dev side, not the mock server. Reviewers seeing red owner specs should re-run serially before filing. I left no footprint: no source edits, scratch dist dir deleted, and I reverted the tsconfig.scratch.json entry my build added. The web tree's other modified files (e2e/categories.spec.ts, e2e/mock-api/server.mjs, categories pages, category-badge.tsx, and a .next-e2e-3221 tsconfig.scratch entry) belong to a concurrent session working TASK-WEB-CAT623 — untouched. I did not move the card's status; board writes are the orchestrator's. The card's own fix list is closed and its acceptance criteria (1–6) all verified, so from the dev side this is ready for the code review + design review it still owes.
- **Description**: ## The bug (verified in code) On `hatiwal-web/src/app/[locale]/listings/[id]/page.tsx` an ACTIVE listing renders `StartConversationButton`, `SellerPhoneReveal`, `SaveButton`, `HideListingButton` and `ReportButton` — and every single one returns `null` for the listing's own seller (`start-conversation-button.tsx:57`, `seller-phone-reveal.tsx:28`, `save-button.tsx:59`, `hide-listing-button.tsx:27`, `report-button.tsx:55`). Since `isActive` is true, the `else` notice branch never renders either. So a seller who opens their own listing (from their own share link, Google, a category page, the similar rail) gets an EMPTY action column whose only surviving element is `<SafetyTips>` — buyer 'how to meet safely' guidance shown to the seller about their own item. `listing.detail.ownListingNotice` already exists in all three web locale files and is never rendered anywhere. ## Build Create `hatiwal-web/src/components/listing/owner-listing-bar.tsx` (`"use client"`, uses `useAuth()` from `@/components/auth/auth-provider` since the page is a Server Component). Props: `listingId`, `sellerId`, `status`, `expiresAt`, `expired`, `viewsCount`, `savesCount`. Returns `null` unless `status === "authed" && user?.id === sellerId` — so buyers see zero change. When it IS the owner, render one bordered `bg-card` panel: - Heading row: `listing.detail.ownListingNotice` (reuse the existing key) + `<StatusBadge status={status}/>` + `<ExpiryBadge status expiresAt expired/>` (reuse `@/components/shared/expiry-badge`, already gates on `status === "active"` and >7 days). - Buttons: primary `Link href={"/my-listings/" + listingId}` → `listing.management` / manage (the existing `ManageListingView` with publish/reserve/sold/renew/delete + views chart); outline `Link href={"/listings/" + listingId + "/edit"}` → `common.edit`; ghost `Link href={"/conversations?listing=" + listingId}` → the buyer conversations for this listing (`/conversations` already reads `?listing=` — `conversations/page.tsx:21-31`). - Use `@/i18n/navigation` `Link` (never `next/link`) so the locale prefix is kept. Wire it into `page.tsx`: place it directly above the `isActive ? … : …` action block, and pass `ownerId={listing.seller?.id}` to `<SafetyTips>` so it hides for the owner (add the same `ownerId`/`useAuth` guard already used by `HideListingButton`). ## API No endpoint or contract change. `GET /listings/:id` already returns `expiresAt`, `expired`, `viewsCount`, `savesCount` (`src/lib/types.ts:141-142`). ## i18n Reuse `listing.detail.ownListingNotice`, `listing.management`, `common.edit`. Add only what is genuinely new (e.g. `listing.detail.ownerViewConversations`) to `messages/en.json`, `ps.json`, `fa.json`. ## Mobile mirror (note, not this task) Mobile `src/screens/shared/ListingDetail.tsx:906-926` shows a bare `ownListingNotice` banner with no Manage/Edit action either — the same bar should later be added there; do NOT build it in this card.
- **Acceptance**: 1) Signed in as the seller, open your own ACTIVE listing at /en/listings/<id>: an owner panel shows the 'this is your listing' notice, the status badge, the expiry pill when <=7 days out, and three working links (Manage → /my-listings/<id>, Edit → /listings/<id>/edit, Conversations → /conversations?listing=<id>). 2) SafetyTips no longer renders for the owner. 3) Signed in as any OTHER user, and as a guest, the page is byte-for-byte unchanged (no owner panel, Message/Offer/Save/Report/SafetyTips all still render). 4) Owner view also works for draft/reserved/sold/expired: the panel renders, ExpiryBadge stays hidden for non-active. 5) All new strings exist in en/ps/fa; verified in /ps and /fa with correct RTL button order and in dark mode. 6) npx tsc --noEmit clean and npm run build passes.

WHAT IS ON DISK (all absolute paths): - /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/account/profile-view.tsx — RatingDisplay in the UserIdentity `meta` slot under the user's name, wrapped in a thumb-sized (min-h-10) link to #my-reviews whose accessible name is score + count + destination; a `secondary` "View my public profile" button in the quick-actions grid → Link `/sellers/{user.id}` via @/i18n/navigation (correctly hidden when `deletionScheduledAt` is set, because Rails scopes the public profile to `User.publicly_active` and it would 404); and the shared ReviewsSection rendered at `#my-reviews` with `title={t("reviews.myReviewsTitle")}` + `headingSize="sm"`. - /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/seller/reviews-section.tsx — gained the optional `title` prop (defaults to `t("sectionTitle")`, so /sellers/[id] is unchanged) plus `headingSize`. Not forked; role tabs, TanStack useInfiniteQuery load-more, skeleton and role-specific EmptyState all reused. - /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/shared/rating-display.tsx, .../shared/user-identity.tsx (`meta` + `nameAs`), messages/{en,ps,fa}.json. ACCEPTANCE VERIFIED: 1-3) Green in e2e — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/e2e/profile.spec.ts has four REP815 specs (rating link with score+count+destination and a >=40px hit box, "My reviews" heading, role toggle → "No buyer reviews yet", public-profile navigation to /sellers/1, link hidden when deletion is scheduled, and a zero-review account showing "No reviews yet" once with no NaN/0.0). 4) /sellers/[id] keeps the default "Ratings & Reviews" heading (asserted in the spec). 5) `reviews.myReviewsTitle` exists in en/ps/fa and is byte-identical to mobile's src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/reviews.json ("My reviews" / "زما نظرونه" / "نظرهای من"); `profile.viewPublicProfile` and `reviews.emptyAsSeller`/`emptyAsBuyer` also in all three. RTL: grepped every touched file for ml-/mr-/pl-/pr-/left-/right-/text-left/text-right and hex colors — zero hits (tokens + logical properties only, so ps/fa mirror correctly and dark works). /ps/profile is additionally fenced by e2e/i18n-digits.spec.ts. 6) `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. `npm run build` exit 0 (79 pages). NOTE for future cycles: a bare `npx next build` FAILS here with "<Html> should not be imported outside of pages/_document" on /404 — that is purely this shell exporting NODE_ENV=development; the npm script sets NODE_ENV=production and builds fine. Not a code defect. TEST RUN: 18/18 Playwright tests passed (e2e/profile.spec.ts + e2e/i18n-digits.spec.ts, isolated ports 3213/4013). That means /sellers/[id] lost a *duplicate* lg score that used to render inside the section; its header score predates this card and is intact. Tree left clean: no uncommitted diff, no tsconfig.json/tsconfig.scratch.json churn, and my two scratch dirs (.next-rep815, .next-e2e-3213) deleted. Pre-existing .next-* dirs from other concurrent sessions were left alone.
- **Description**: ## The gap (verified in code)
`hatiwal-web/src/components/account/profile-view.tsx` (read in full) shows three stat tiles (active/sold/saved), info rows, and four quick-action buttons. There is **no rating, no review count, no way to read the reviews buyers left about you, and no link to your own public profile**. A seller on web literally cannot see their own reputation — the one number that drives whether buyers message them. Mobile `src/screens/shared/Profile.tsx:292` has a 'Reviews' quick action → `AllReviews` with buyer/seller tabs, and shows the rating in the header.

The data is already there and unused: `UserSerializer` `:me` view serves `avg_rating` + `review_count` (`hatiwal-api/app/serializers/user_serializer.rb:60-61`), and `hatiwal-web/src/lib/types.ts:56-57` already declares `avgRating`/`reviewCount` on `User`. Only the UI is missing.

## Build
Edit `hatiwal-web/src/components/account/profile-view.tsx`:
1. Put `<RatingDisplay avgRating={user.avgRating} reviewCount={user.reviewCount} size="lg"/>` (reuse `@/components/shared/rating-display`) under the `UserIdentity` block. It already renders a graceful 'no reviews yet' state when `avgRating` is null.
2. Add a 'View my public profile' secondary button to the existing quick-actions grid → `Link href={"/sellers/" + user.id}` (that route is the canonical public profile; `/users/[id]` redirects to it). This closes the current dead end where a seller cannot see how buyers see them.
3. Render the reviews list on your own profile by reusing `@/components/seller/reviews-section` — `<ReviewsSection sellerId={user.id} avgRating={user.avgRating} reviewCount={user.reviewCount ?? 0}/>`. It already has the of_seller/of_buyer `SegmentedControl`, TanStack Query load-more, skeleton and `EmptyState`, and `getUserReviews` is a public endpoint so it works for your own id unchanged.
4. Add an optional `title` prop to `reviews-section.tsx` (default = current `t("reviews.sectionTitle")`) and pass `reviews.myReviewsTitle` from the profile so the heading reads 'My reviews' instead of 'Ratings & reviews'. Do not fork the component.

## API
No endpoint or contract change. Reuses `GET /users/:id/reviews?role=of_seller|of_buyer` (already in `src/lib/api/reviews.ts`) and the existing `/api/me` payload.

## i18n
Add `reviews.myReviewsTitle` (mirror the mobile key/wording verbatim) and one key for the public-profile button (e.g. `profile.viewPublicProfile`) to `messages/en.json`, `ps.json`, `fa.json`. `reviews.myReviewsTitle` is currently missing from all three web locale files.
- **Acceptance**: 1) /en/profile shows your star rating + review count under your name, and a 'My reviews' section with the As-seller / As-buyer toggle, load-more, skeleton while loading, and the empty state when you have none. 2) A brand-new account with zero reviews shows the graceful no-rating state and the reviews empty state — never 'NaN', '0 stars' or a blank block. 3) A 'View my public profile' button navigates to /sellers/<your id> and back. 4) /sellers/[id] is visually unchanged (ReviewsSection default title still 'Ratings & reviews' — verified by loading another seller's page). 5) reviews.myReviewsTitle + the new profile key exist in en/ps/fa; checked in /ps and /fa (RTL star order, RTL segmented control) and in dark mode. 6) npx tsc --noEmit clean and npm run build passes.

## TASK-WEB-CAT623

- **Type**: web
- **Priority**: P2
