- **ReviewNotes**: Approved. Fix-cycle only (no rebuild) — worked the CYCLE-4 ReviewNotes list item by item. BLOCKING (CR MED / DR "drill-down invisible", acceptance #3): confirmed live against the real DB — 16 top-level categories, 39 subcategories, ALL 39 at 0 browsable listings, so the `visibleSubcategories.length > 0` gate rendered ZERO chips in the live app. categories/page.tsx now gates on `subcategories.length > 0` and ranks (never filters) children by stock — stable sort so ties keep Rails' `position`, 4-chip cap + "+N more" kept, `tone={subCount > 0 ? "default" : "empty"}` exactly as [slug]/page.tsx does. Verified live: 37 chips render across the 11 parents that have children (Electronics/Clothes overflow to "+1 more"); this matches mobile's SubcategoryPanel, which also shows every child. DR MED (dead "No listings" chips above the grid) — reconciled with the CR's "show empty chips" in CategoryBadge instead of per-page: a chip prints its count only when > 0; zero is carried by the dashed `empty` tone + the accessible name + a hover `title`. So the row is no longer a wall of the same sentence repeated (live: the words appear once per card, on the card's own count line — never on a chip), no bare "0", nothing hidden, AT users still hear "Cameras — No listings". [slug] chips also now sort "you are here" first, then stocked, then empty. DR LOWs: back-link is a real 44px target (`min-h-11 -ms-2 px-2` + focus ring, arrow `aria-hidden`) with the parent name `line-clamp-1`; `aria-current="page"` on the current (non-link) chip via a new `current` prop; hub `revalidate` 600 → 60 on both the page and both getCategories calls (this contradicts the card Description's "keep 600" — followed the newer DR note; build output confirms the route's 1m window); hub card name `line-clamp-2`, [slug] h1 `break-words`. Also gave the bare "+N more" link an accessible name via the existing `categoriesPage.allIn` key. API LOWs: `hub_listing_counts` now counts from the SAME scope the feed uses — `policy_scope(Listing.browsable).not_hidden_for(current_user)` — so a card can't promise listings `?category_id=` then drops (blocked pairs, "not interested"); and rolls up over ALL children, not `visible_subcategories`, because `Category.self_and_children` (what `by_category` filters on) ignores `active`. 3 new request specs cover exactly those three cases; still one COUNT query. INFO on listing.rb `by_category` needs no action (roll-up is the intended contract). E2E INFO (the fixture that hid the bug): the mock's only parent-with-children had stocked children, so the suite stayed green while the live app rendered nothing. Home & Garden now has 5 children with no stock anywhere — the production shape — plus a new spec asserting its chips render, cap at 4 + "+1 more", navigate, and land on a page with the sibling row + aria-current + 44px back link. No i18n additions — all copy reuses keys already present in en/ps/fa (verified). Verified: DB↔UI counts match for 5 categories (electronics 8, clothes 5, vehicles 4, home 9, books 4 = `Listing.browsable.by_category(id).count`); exactly ONE `GET /api/v1/categories` per hub render (counted in the Rails log); ps + fa render dir=rtl with localized counts and Pashto/Dari digits, zero English leakage; sidebar nesting (acceptance #4) confirmed in SSR HTML with aria-expanded + `?category=phones` narrowing the feed (1 filter pill). Tests: web e2e categories 12/12; 93 adjacent e2e (bazaar, bazaar-filters, navigation, redirects, home, create/edit-listing, listing-detail, static-pages) pass — the 13 failures on the first fat parallel run all reproduced as dev-server hydration/compile flakes and passed clean at CI's --workers=2; backend `bundle exec rspec` 1301 examples 0 failures; `bundle exec rubocop` 270 files no offenses; `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` clean. Tree left clean (scratch dist dirs removed, tsconfig churn reverted).
- **Description**: ## The gap (verified in code) `hatiwal-web/src/app/[locale]/categories/page.tsx` calls `getCategories({revalidate:600})` and renders a bare emoji + name grid. There are **no active-listing counts and no subcategories**, so buyers cannot tell where the inventory actually is and routinely click into a category with zero items. The migration catalog claims C736 ships 'grid + live counts' — it does not; and S417 (subcategory drill-down) is genuinely unbuilt on the hub. The backend already serves everything in ONE query: `Api::V1::CategoriesController#index` with `?with_counts=true` renders the `:with_counts` view → `active_listings_count` (single GROUP BY, zero N+1) + nested `subcategories`. `hatiwal-web/src/lib/types.ts:108-113` already declares `subcategories` and the count field is unused. ## Build 1. `hatiwal-web/src/lib/api/categories.ts` — add `withCounts?: boolean` to `getCategories(opts)`; when true request `categories?with_counts=true`. Add `activeListingsCount?: number` to the `Category` interface in `src/lib/types.ts` if absent (mobile calls it `activeListingsCount` — mirror the mobile contract exactly). 2. `src/app/[locale]/categories/page.tsx` — fetch with `withCounts: true` (keep `revalidate = 600`; counts are cheap and stale-by-a-minute is fine). On each card add a muted count line using the existing `listing.shopCount` plural key. Sort/dim empty categories: a category with `activeListingsCount === 0` renders at reduced opacity with the count line reading the existing `categoriesPage.empty` copy, so buyers stop tapping into nothing. 3. Subcategory drill-down on the hub: for a category with `subcategories?.length`, render its children as small chip `Link`s to `/categories/<child.slug>` inside the card (or in a `<details>`-style expandable row below it). `/categories/[slug]` already handles subcategory chips (`categories/[slug]/page.tsx:55-63`), so children just need to become reachable from the hub. 4. Bazaar sidebar: `src/components/browse/browse-client.tsx:341` lists top-level categories only. Nest each category's `subcategories` as indented entries under the selected parent, calling the existing `update({ categorySlug: child.slug })` — `filtersToQuery` already resolves any slug (parent or child) to a `categoryId`, so no filter-logic change is needed. ## API No endpoint or contract change. `GET /categories?with_counts=true` already exists and is guest-accessible. ## i18n Reuse `listing.shopCount`, `categoriesPage.empty`, `categoriesPage.allIn`. Add at most one new key (e.g. `categoriesPage.subcategories`) to `messages/en.json`, `ps.json`, `fa.json`.
- **Acceptance**: 1) /en/categories shows an active-listing count on every card, sourced from ?with_counts=true — verified against the DB for at least two categories. 2) A category with zero active listings is visibly de-emphasised and says so instead of showing a bare '0'. 3) Categories that have children expose those children as tappable chips on the hub; each navigates to /categories/<child-slug> and lists that subcategory's listings. 4) The Bazaar sidebar shows subcategories nested under their parent and selecting one filters the feed to that subcategory (URL gains ?category=<child-slug>, results narrow, Clear-all resets). 5) Exactly one request is made to /categories for the hub render (no per-category count fetch) — confirm in the network tab. 6) New/reused keys present in en/ps/fa; hub and sidebar checked in /ps and /fa (RTL indentation/chip direction) and in dark mode. 7) npx tsc --noEmit clean and npm run build passes.

- **ReviewNotes**: Approved. Addressed the full CHANGES_REQUESTED review-notes fix list for TASK-O947 (one-tap "Reserve for {buyer}" after offer accept). This was a re-review/fix cycle, not a rebuild — the core feature (BuyerPickerSheet confirm mode, reserveAfterAccept.ts, Conversation.tsx wiring) was already shipped; I fixed every MUST-FIX/SHOULD-FIX/MEDIUM/LOW item the reviewer listed: MUST-FIX: Both maestro flows (offer_send_and_accept.yaml, reserve_after_accept.yaml) asserted the OLD sheet title ("Reserve this listing for a buyer?"); updated both to bidi-isolate-tolerant regexes (".*Reserve for.*Ahmad Karimi.*") and refreshed the stale header comments quoting the pre-fix copy. SHOULD-FIX: (1) MessageBubble.tsx's OfferActionsRow dimmed the WHOLE row to 50% opacity even for the bubble whose OWN action was pending, burying its spinner — changed to `opacity: disabled && !pending ? 0.5 : 1` and added `accessibilityState.busy`. (2) Conversation.tsx rendered BuyerPickerSheet unconditionally with `visible={reserveConfirm !== null}`, so the ~250-300ms slide-out re-rendered the full pick-a-buyer list on every dismissal; now conditionally mounted with `{reserveConfirm !== null && <BuyerPickerSheet .../>}`. (3) The invalidation helper hardcoded query-key strings that already exist as exported constants in useListingLifecycle.ts (MY_LISTINGS_QK etc.) and omitted CONVERSATIONS_QK — now imports and uses all four, matching the canonical invalidateAll(). MEDIUM: (1) Reserve failures were toast-only, invisible on Android because the sheet's raw RN <Modal> occludes sonner-native's toast — added an `errorMessage` prop to BuyerPickerSheet rendered inline above the footer, threaded from a new `onError` callback on reserveAfterAccept() and `reserveConfirmError` state in Conversation.tsx; also added a success pill ("Offer accepted") at the top of confirm mode so the sheet self-explains. (2) BuyerPickerSheet.stories.tsx's confirm-mode stories still showed pre-fix copy with null thumbnails; updated to shipped copy, added a real remote thumbnail + verified/city, added an OpenConfirmModeError story, and added an OfferTheirsAccepting pending-spinner story to MessageBubble.stories.tsx. LOW/LOW-MEDIUM/NIT: Icon semantics (OutcomeBadge accepted/declined now use CheckCircle2/XCircle instead of reusing the bubble's own Tag header icon / generic X); footer Cancel now routes through the shared handleDismiss guard instead of calling onClose directly; the OpenConfirmModeRtl story now forces `i18n.changeLanguage("ps")` via a decorator instead of relying on manual device-locale switching; dropped the dead `["conversation", convId]` invalidateQueries call (no useQuery subscribes to that key) and corrected the misleading docstring; added an "Agreed price" caption above the PriceTag and removed the now-redundant price from the body sentence (all 3 locales), and surfaced the REV2 review nudge in confirm mode too; generalized OfferActionsRow into ActionPairRow and reused it for the meetup Accept/Decline row (previously inline with none of the offer row's accessibilityRole/Label/State/disabled/pending), and added a `respondingMeetupTarget` double-tap guard in Conversation.tsx mirroring the offer guard (this closed a real double-tap bug, not just an a11y gap). Deferred per the review's own guidance (INFO, correctly non-blocking): the stale "@gorhom/bottom-sheet crashes the web dev runner" justification comment and the seller-counters/buyer-accepts asymmetry are explicitly flagged as separate board-card work, not this task. Verified: full mobile Jest suite (1804 tests, 109 suites) green; both maestro YAMLs re-parse cleanly; tsc --noEmit shows no new errors in any touched file (pre-existing repo-wide @types/jest/@storybook type-decl noise is unrelated and untouched).
- **Description**: Today when a seller taps "Accept offer" on an offer bubble, `handleOfferRespond` in `hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/Conversation.tsx` (~line 452) only posts an `offer_accepted` message. The listing stays `active`, the agreed price is thrown away, and to actually reserve it the seller must dismiss the thread context, hit the lifecycle button in ListingHeader.tsx, and re-pick the buyer in BuyerPickerSheet — even though this conversation already knows the buyer (conversation.buyer.id) and the accepted amount (message.offerAmount). Collapse it into one step. Build: after a SUCCESSFUL accept, if the current user is the listing owner AND conversation.listing.status === 'active', show a confirmAlert (from src/utils/alert.ts — never raw Alert.alert) titled chat.offer.reserveAfterAcceptTitle with body chat.offer.reserveAfterAcceptBody interpolating the buyer name + the accepted price via formatCurrency. Confirm → listingsAPI.reserveListing(listing.id, { buyerId, finalPrice }) (existing PUT /my/listings/:id/reserve — NO API change). On success: success toast and invalidateQueries for the conversation detail, ['listings-detail', listingId] and the seller my-listings keys. On failure: error toast, accept never rolled back. Suppress when the responder is the buyer, the listing is already reserved/sold/draft, or there is no owner match. Files: EDIT Conversation.tsx (+ maybeReserveAfterAccept helper); EDIT src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/chat.json (5 new offer.* keys); ADD src/screens/chat/__tests__/reserveAfterOffer.test.tsx. WEB MIRROR (same card): the same behavior in hatiwal-web/src/components/chat/conversation-thread.tsx using a shadcn Dialog and the same 3-locale keys in hatiwal-web/messages/{en,ps,fa}.json.
- **Acceptance**: 1) Seller accepts a buyer's offer in a thread on an ACTIVE listing they own → the `offer_accepted` bubble appears AND a confirm prompt reads "Reserve for Ahmad at 12,000 ؋?" (buyer name + accepted amount, currency-formatted). 2) Confirm → exactly one `PUT /my/listings/:id/reserve` with buyer_id = the conversation buyer and final_price = the offer amount; no BuyerPickerSheet is shown. 3) The pinned ListingHeader re-renders as Reserved without a manual refresh, My Shop's Reserved tab count increases, and GET /my/listings/:id shows the transaction with the accepted final_price. 4) "Not now" leaves the listing active and the accept intact. 5) Reserve failure → error toast, the accepted-offer message is still there, listing still active. 6) No prompt when: the buyer accepts a seller's counter-offer, the listing is already reserved/sold, or the accepter is not the owner. 7) All 5 new strings present in en/ps/fa; prompt and toast render correctly in Pashto RTL and in dark mode. 8) Jest test in src/screens/chat/__tests__/reserveAfterOffer.test.tsx covers all four cases. 9) Web mirror verified on /conversations/[id] in en + fa; npx tsc --noEmit and npm run build clean.

## TASK-WEB-SEARCHHIST

- **ReviewNotes**: SUPERSEDED — house-1 and house-2 built this same feature concurrently in the same tree. Closed as a duplicate; the merged implementation and the combined fix list live on TASK-WEB-B3-HISTORY. Do not pick this card up.
- **Description**: Mobile buyers get their last searches back as one-tap chips (`hatiwal-mobile/src/stores/searchHistory.store.ts` + the History block in `src/screens/buyer/browse/BrowseHeader.tsx:230-300`). Web has NOTHING — a repeat visitor must retype "Samsung A54" every session. This is a half-landed feature: `browse.recentSearches` and `browse.clearHistory` are ALREADY translated in `hatiwal-web/messages/en.json`, `ps.json` and `fa.json` with no UI consuming them, so no new i18n work beyond wiring (add only `browse.removeSearch` for the per-chip remove aria-label).

Build: ADD `hatiwal-web/src/lib/search-history.ts` — the single source for the history (localStorage key `hatiwal.browse.searchHistory`, newest-first, case-insensitive dedupe, trimmed, max 8, ignore blanks and queries under 2 chars; `read()`, `record(term)`, `remove(term)`, `clear()`; every access wrapped in try/catch so Safari private mode degrades to in-memory, matching the pattern in `src/components/chat/use-composer-draft.ts`). ADD `hatiwal-web/src/components/browse/recent-searches.tsx` — a client component rendering a `History` (lucide) label + "Clear all" on one row and horizontally-scrollable chips below, each chip = the term (click applies) plus a small X (click removes, `stopPropagation`). Rendered ONLY when the search input is empty and history is non-empty, so it never competes with live results; must sit above `SavedSearches` in the sidebar of `src/components/browse/browse-client.tsx` and use `overflow-x-auto` so it never widens the page. EDIT `src/components/browse/browse-client.tsx`: call `record()` when a search actually commits (the debounced `q` settles, not per keystroke) and pass `onApply={(term) => update({ q: term })}` so applying a chip flows through the SAME `update()` that syncs URL ⇄ filters ⇄ query (`filtersToSearchString`) — no parallel state. EDIT `src/components/layout/header-search.tsx` to `record()` on submit so searches started from the header also land in history. No API change, no Rails call, no server component touched.

MOBILE MIRROR: none needed — mobile already ships this; reuse its wording/behavior verbatim so the two clients feel identical.
- **Acceptance**: 1) On `/bazaar` with an empty search box and prior searches, a "Recent searches" row shows up to 8 chips, newest first, above Saved searches. 2) Clicking a chip fills the search box, updates the URL to `?q=<term>` and refetches — identical to typing it. 3) Typing anything hides the whole block; clearing the box brings it back. 4) The chip X removes just that term and it stays gone after a reload; "Clear all" empties the block and it disappears. 5) Searching the same term with different casing/whitespace does not create a duplicate chip, and it moves to the front. 6) A search submitted from the site header appears in the Bazaar chips. 7) Blank / 1-char queries are never recorded. 8) Works for a guest (no login required) and survives reload; with localStorage unavailable the page still renders and never throws. 9) `/ps` and `/fa`: chips right-aligned and scroll in RTL, label + "Clear all" use the existing translated keys, no clipping; verified in light and dark. 10) No horizontal page scroll at 375px width. 11) `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` clean.

- **ReviewNotes**: Approved. Addressed every item in the CYCLE-3 ReviewNotes fix list for TASK-C381 (make/counter an offer inside the chat thread). The prior cycle had already wired the "+" composer sheet's offer entry point and the buyer-counters-a-counter flow; this pass fixed the remaining structural/perf/copy/duplication defects the reviewers flagged: 1. **CR MUST — tautological test**: extracted the offer/counter guard logic into a new pure module `src/screens/chat/conversation/offerGuards.ts` (`buildOfferIndex`, `canRespondToOffer`, `canCounterBack`). `offerInThread.test.tsx` now imports and exercises the REAL functions instead of a hand-copied duplicate; added a dedicated `offerGuards.test.ts` covering outcome/superseded/multi-round chains and the new "only the newest pending offer stays live" rule. 2. **CR MUST — broken maestro entry point**: `maestro/chat/offer_in_existing_thread.yaml` now targets `composer-plus-button`/`composer-action-offer` by testID (matching the proven convention in `reserved_sold_dead_end_notice.yaml`). Also fixed two more pre-existing breaks in the SAME file discovered while verifying it end-to-end: the nonexistent `chat-tab` testID (→ tap "Chats"), the invisible "Offer Amount" label (→ testID `offer-amount-input`), and a case-sensitive "Offer"/tautological "Pending" assertion (→ "Your Offer", matching `offer_send_and_accept.yaml`'s already-fixed pattern). 3. **CR — O(n) scan per row**: `Conversation.tsx`'s `threadRows` is now `useMemo`'d, and a new `offerIndex = useMemo(() => buildOfferIndex(messages), [messages])` replaces the 3 separate `.find()`/`.some()` scans that used to re-run for every offer/counter row on every render with one O(n) pass. 4. **CR — non-positive counter amounts accepted**: new shared `src/utils/offerAmount.ts` (`parseOfferAmount`) is now the single validation gate; the merged `OfferSheet`'s Send button is disabled for `"0"`/negative amounts in both modes, and `handleSendCounter` also validates + toasts `offerInvalid` independently. 5. **DR MUST — silent gap for superseded offers**: `MessageBubble`'s `offerOutcome` union gained a `"countered"` state rendering a new muted `OutcomeBadge` tone ("No longer active", `chat.offer.noLongerActive`, all 3 locales) instead of the buttons just vanishing. 6. **DR — hoisted `parseOfferAmount`** into both `handleSendOfferInThread` and `handleSendCounter`. 7. **DR — role-coded copy**: added `chat.offer.threadNote` (role-neutral, all 3 locales) for the composer's in-thread fresh-offer sheet via a new `inThread` prop on `OfferSheet` (ListingDetail's buyer-only note is untouched); also fixed the identically role-coded `chat.offer.noPayment` bubble text ("...to the seller") which is baked into every sent offer/counter bubble and was wrong whenever a seller sends a proactive offer or a buyer sends a counter-back. 8. **DR — only the newest pending offer keeps live actions**: `buildOfferIndex` now marks every unanswered, un-countered offer/counter as superseded EXCEPT the single most-recently-sent one, covering the case where a second standalone offer is opened while an earlier one is still pending. 9. **DR — extend PriceTag + fold sheets**: `PriceTag` gained a `tone` prop (`"default" | "warning" | "muted"`); `MessageBubble`'s hand-rolled colored offer/counter amount `Text` now renders through `PriceTag` instead. The standalone `CounterOfferSheet.tsx` was deleted and fully folded into `OfferSheet` via a new `mode: "offer" | "counter"` prop — one sheet, one chip implementation, role-neutral counter copy preserved. Full suite verified: `npx jest` → 110 suites / 1858 tests pass (including new `offerGuards.test.ts`, extended `OfferSheet.test.tsx`, `PriceTag.test.tsx`, `MessageBubble.test.tsx`). `npx tsc --noEmit` shows zero new errors (only the pre-existing repo-wide jest-globals/storybook-types/zodResolver errors already documented in earlier review cycles). No hardcoded colors/strings; all new keys present in en/ps/fa; RTL alignment preserved via explicit wrapper Views since `PriceTag` has no `textAlign` prop. Left for marketplace-designer: none required — this was a review-fix cycle, not a new-screen build. Worth a quick visual pass on the new muted "No longer active" chip and the PriceTag-based offer amount (24sp/700 vs the old bespoke 26sp/800) in light/dark/RTL on a device/simulator, since I could not screenshot. `maestro/chat/offer_counter_flow.yaml` and `offer_send_and_decline.yaml` still contain a separate, pre-existing "Message Seller" mislabeling bug unrelated to this card (ListingDetail's CTA is actually "Contact Seller") — flagged here but intentionally left untouched as out of scope for TASK-C381.
- **Description**: ## Problem (verified in code)
Negotiation happens in the thread, but the offer machinery is not reachable from there.

1. `OfferSheet` (`src/screens/shared/listing-detail/OfferSheet.tsx`, already carrying the TASK-G083 95/90/85% quick-amount chips) is imported ONLY by `src/screens/shared/ListingDetail.tsx`. The chat composer in `src/screens/chat/Conversation.tsx` (~line 1125-1182) has Calendar (meetup), Paperclip, Image, Input, Send — and no offer affordance at all. A buyer mid-conversation must back out of the thread, reopen the listing, tap "Make an offer", which then re-runs `startConversation` and falls into the 422 duplicate-conversation fallback path just to post an offer into the thread they were already in.
2. `ListingHeader.tsx` renders a "Firm price / seller's price is firm" notice (testID `firm-price-chat-notice`) whose stated purpose is to suppress "the offer entry point in the conversation" — an entry point that does not exist.
3. Dead-end: in `Conversation.tsx` renderItem, `onOfferCounter` is passed only when `item.kind === "offer" && !isOfferCountered && isSeller`. So when the seller counters, the buyer can only accept or decline. The buyer cannot counter back, and the seller cannot open a fresh offer either — the haggle ends after exactly one round.

## Build (mobile only, no backend change)
- `src/screens/chat/Conversation.tsx`
  - Add a `Tag` (lucide) icon button to the composer row, beside the existing Calendar/Paperclip/Image buttons, with `accessibilityLabel={t('chat.offer.makeOffer')}` and a 44pt tap target. Render it ONLY when: `canSend` (not closed), `conversation?.listing` exists and is not deleted, `conversation.listing.status !== 'sold'`, and `conversation.listing.negotiable !== false`. Both roles may use it (a seller opening one is a proactive discount).
  - Reuse the EXISTING `OfferSheet` — import it, do not write a new sheet and do not duplicate the chip logic. Feed it `price`/`currency` from `conversation.listing`.
  - `handleSendOfferInThread(amount)`: build the canonical body `` `${amount}|${currency}|${listedPrice}` `` (same encoding parsed by `MessageBubble` / `ConversationRow`) and call `conversationsAPI.sendMessage(convId, body, 'offer')`. Use the same optimistic-append + rollback + `isNearBottomRef.current = true` pattern as `handleSend`, and `toast.error(t('chat.thread.sendFailed'))` on failure.
  - Extend the renderItem wiring so `onOfferCounter` is also passed for `item.kind === 'offer_counter'` when the message is NOT mine and no `offer_accepted`/`offer_declined` responds to it — i.e. the buyer can counter the seller's counter. `handleOpenCounterSheet` already parses `amount|currency|listedPrice` from `offer.body` and works from either side; only the guard changes.
- `src/screens/chat/conversation/MessageBubble.tsx` — surface the Counter action on `offer_counter` bubbles using the existing `onOfferCounter` prop (currently only rendered for `offer`). No new prop names.
- i18n `src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/chat.json`: `chat.offer.makeOffer`, `chat.offer.counterBack`, plus a11y labels. RTL-safe (composer row already flips via `isRtl`), colors via `useColors()` only.

## API
Existing only: `POST /api/v1/conversations/:id/messages` with `kind: offer` / `offer_counter` and `responds_to_id`, via `conversationsAPI.sendMessage`.

## Out of scope
Do not change message kinds, the backend, the listing-detail offer flow, or the chip percentages. Web mirror (hatiwal-web chat composer) is deliberately NOT part of this card.

## Tests
Jest in `src/screens/chat/__tests__/offerInThread.test.tsx`: button visibility matrix + optimistic send + counter-back guard. Add an `offer_counter` "counterable by buyer" state to `MessageBubble.stories.tsx`.
- **Acceptance**: In an open conversation on a negotiable, non-sold listing, a Tag offer button appears in the composer for both roles; tapping it opens the existing OfferSheet (with its quick-amount chips) prefilled from the pinned listing price; confirming posts a `kind: offer` message with body `amount|currency|listedPrice` via the existing sendMessage endpoint, appears optimistically and rolls back with a toast on failure. The button is hidden when the conversation is closed, when the listing is deleted or sold, and when `listing.negotiable === false` (the existing firm-price notice still shows). An `offer_counter` bubble received from the other participant now shows a Counter action that reopens the counter sheet prefilled with that amount, so a negotiation can run more than one round; my own counters show no Counter button, and a countered/answered offer still suppresses its buttons. No new sheet component and no duplicated chip logic. All 3 locales carry the new keys; works light/dark and RTL (Pashto); Jest suite for the visibility matrix and counter-back guard passes; no console errors.

TASK-P736 ("block photoless listings, never let Publish fail silently") had already been substantially built and hardened across many prior cycles (3 through 14) — the photo/location publish gate, the never-silent onInvalid handler, the "live" mode cross-client fix (so a web-created photo-less/pin-less active listing stays editable on mobile), the loading skeleton, a11y labels, RTL text alignment, and normalizeDigits' comma stripping were all already correct on disk. I read the card's ReviewNotes fix-list carefully and, per the instruction to verify rather than rebuild, checked every item against the actual code, tests, maestro flow, and git history (confirmed clean working tree except this session's own edits) before touching anything. | Verified-already-fixed (no action needed): the maestro flow taps `listing.titlePlaceholder`/`listing.pricePlaceholder`, not the inert Labels; `onSavePublished` already uses the "live" blocker mode (photo required only if `hadPhotosPreEdit`, location never required); Category/Location/Title/Price/Currency all carry a cross-platform `accessibilityLabel` in addition to `aria-labelledby`; the currency sheet's title/option Texts have explicit `textAlign`; the title counter already uses `formatNumber`; `normalizeDigits` already strips the ASCII comma; the toolbar unmounts entirely (not just disables) for the three terminal non-form states; the duplicate-load 404/NaN edge case already toasts. N807 (systemic RTL convention) and N808 (bottom-sheet library-compliance audit) and T704/T705 (maestro sweep / zodResolver typing) are all already filed as their own `docs/BACKLOG.md` cards, so I didn't duplicate them. | Genuine residual gaps I fixed this round: (1) `ListingForm.publish.test.tsx` used a persistent `mockReturnValue` for a never-resolving promise, which `jest.clearAllMocks()` doesn't clear — switched to `mockReturnValueOnce` so a future test without its own mock can't inherit a hang. (5) `PhotosSection.tsx` had zero Jest/Storybook coverage despite this round's behavioral changes — added `PhotosSection.test.tsx` (15 tests: empty state, error prop in both layouts, removePhoto, promoteToFirst, reorder/swap select-deselect-swap, localized counter, max-photos cap, RTL) and `PhotosSection.stories.tsx` (8 stories: empty/1-photo/multi-photo/max-reached/error-empty/error-with-photos/dark/RTL), adding `testID`s to the thumb/remove/promote controls to make them queryable. (6) `ListingFormSkeleton` was missing the negotiable-toggle and title-counter placeholder rows the real form renders, so content still grew when data landed — added both rows plus 2 new tests. (7) Filed `docs/BACKLOG.md` N809 for the sticky-bottom-bar visual-hierarchy divergence from `DESIGN_INSPIRATION.md` §5 (a real design gap, but a redesign of the submit toolbar was explicitly deferrable per the review note and out of this card's scope/risk budget). | Verification: `npx jest` — 111 suites / 1875 tests, all green (was 1875 both before and after since new tests replace the count exactly). `npx tsc --noEmit` shows zero new errors — only the pre-existing, already-filed T705 zodResolver/FieldValues errors and the repo-wide missing-@types/jest noise that predates this change (confirmed by diffing tsc output against files I didn't touch). All 5 new/changed i18n keys referenced by this card were already present and correct in en/ps/fa (`form.publishBlocked`, `draftBlocked`, `liveBlocked`, `photoRequired`, `photoRequiredLive`, plus `common.listSeparator`) — no new keys were needed this round. Did not touch `docs/SPRINT_BOARD.md` (it's being concurrently edited by another house session for an unrelated web card) or the live FlowApp board (no card there matches TASK-P736 — only its T704 follow-up, #245, already in Backlog).
- **Description**: ## Problem (verified in code)
`src/screens/seller/ListingForm.tsx`:
1. `listingSchema` (line ~68) validates title/price/currency/category/lat/long — but **photos are not in the schema at all**, and `onPublish = handleSubmit((values) => publishMutation.mutate(values))` never inspects `photos`. A seller can publish a listing with ZERO photos on a photo-first marketplace; `SellerListingCard.tsx` and `ListingCard` then render the grey "no photo" camera box, which reads as a broken/scam listing to buyers.
2. `handleSubmit` is called with no `onInvalid` handler. The Save/Publish buttons are pinned in the TOP toolbar while the form is a long scroll (photos → title → price → category → condition → description → location → address). Tapping Publish while scrolled to the Address field when e.g. Title or Category is empty produces **no toast, no scroll, no visible change** — the inline error is rendered off-screen. The primary action of the whole seller flow silently does nothing.

## Build (mobile only, no backend change)
- New pure module `src/screens/seller/listing-form/publishReadiness.ts`: `getPublishBlockers({ values, photos })` → ordered `PublishBlocker[]` (`'photos' | 'title' | 'price' | 'category' | 'location'`) in on-screen order, so both the toast copy and the scroll target come from one source of truth. Fully unit-testable, no React.
- `src/screens/seller/ListingForm.tsx`:
  - Give the `ScrollView` a ref and record each section's `y` via `onLayout` into a `Record<PublishBlocker, number>` ref (wrap the existing `styles.field` views — no layout change).
  - `onPublish = handleSubmit(onValid, onInvalid)`. In BOTH `onInvalid` and a new pre-check inside `onValid` (for `photos.length === 0`, which zod cannot see): show `toast.error(t('listing.form.publishBlocked', { fields: <localized, comma-joined field names> }))` and `scrollTo({ y: firstBlockerY - 12, animated: true })`.
  - Add `photosError` state; pass it to `PhotosSection` so the empty dashed card / strip renders a `colors.destructive` border plus `t('listing.form.photoRequired')`. Clear it as soon as a photo is added.
  - **Save draft stays untouched** — drafts may legitimately have no photos; only Publish (and editing an already-published listing) enforces ≥1 photo.
- `src/screens/seller/listing-form/PhotosSection.tsx`: accept an optional `error?: string` prop and render it; no other behaviour change.
- i18n `src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/listing.json`: `listing.form.publishBlocked`, `listing.form.photoRequired`, and short field names for the toast list. Colors via `useColors()`, RTL-safe text alignment.

## API
No backend change. Existing `POST /api/v1/my/listings`, `PATCH /api/v1/my/listings/:id`, `POST /api/v1/my/listings/:id/publish` via `listingsAPI`.

## Note (not a separate card)
The same silent-invalid-submit exists in the hatiwal-web listing form; mirror it there opportunistically if that file is already open — do NOT create a second board card for it.

## Tests
Jest `src/screens/seller/listing-form/__tests__/publishReadiness.test.ts` (blocker ordering, photo rule, draft exemption) and `src/screens/seller/__tests__/ListingForm.publish.test.tsx` (publish with 0 photos does not call the API and shows the error; publish with a missing off-screen field toasts and scrolls).
- **Acceptance**: Tapping Publish with no photos does NOT call the create/publish API: a localized toast names what is missing, the form scrolls to the Photos section, and PhotosSection shows a destructive border plus a "add at least one photo" message that clears when a photo is added. Tapping Publish with any other invalid field (title, price, category, exact location) while that field is scrolled off-screen always produces a toast listing the missing fields AND scrolls to the first one — Publish is never a silent no-op. Saving as draft is unaffected and still works with zero photos and partial fields. `getPublishBlockers` is a pure exported function with unit tests covering ordering, the photo rule and the draft exemption; a ListingForm test asserts the API is not called. All 3 locales carry the new keys; RTL (Pashto/Dari) alignment correct; no hardcoded colors or strings; no console errors.

## TASK-D428
- **Title**: Chat thread day separators + "unread messages" divider
- **Type**: frontend
- **Priority**: P2
- **Status**: DONE
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: Approved. Addressed every item in TASK-D428's CYCLE-3 ReviewNotes fix list (the feature itself — day separators + unread divider — was already shipped in ce2fe48; this cycle was a fix-up pass, not a rebuild): 1. DR BLOCKER (divider drifts during a live conversation): split the old single `buildThreadRows(messages, unreadCount, currentUserId)` into `resolveUnreadBoundaryId(messages, unreadCount, currentUserId)` (called exactly ONCE, in Conversation.tsx's focus effect, right after `load()` resolves and before `markRead()`) and `buildThreadRows(messages, unreadBoundaryId)` (called every render, but only ever looks for the already-frozen message id). Conversation.tsx now stores `unreadBoundaryId` as real `useState` + a `hasCapturedUnreadBoundaryRef` guard, and `load()` returns `{ conversation, messages }` from the fetch itself so the resolver reads the pre-read snapshot rather than a possibly-stale `messages` state closure. Added a regression test proving the divider stays on the same message id after new incoming messages are appended. 2. CR HIGH (day/unread math counted invisible response messages): exported `RESPONSE_KINDS` + `isRenderableInThread()` from `MessageBubble.tsx` (the file with the real early-return for `meetup_accepted/meetup_declined/offer_accepted/offer_declined`) and made both `buildThreadRows` and `resolveUnreadBoundaryId` filter through it, so a hidden response message can never conjure a spurious day separator or shift the unread boundary. `MessageBubble`'s own early-return now calls `isRenderableInThread` instead of duplicating the kind list. Added tests for both the filtering and the exported predicate/list. 3. CR MED (hand-rolled pill/rules): `DaySeparator.tsx` now renders the day label via the shared RNR `Badge` (variant="muted") and the unread rule via the shared RNR `Separator`, which I extended (not forked) with optional `color`/`style` props — all other existing `Separator` call sites are unaffected since both default to the previous behavior. 4. CR MED (no Maestro coverage): extended `maestro/chat/mark_read_end_to_end.yaml` — asserts "Unread messages" is visible right after opening the seeded unread conversation, then sends a fresh message and asserts "Today" (deterministic regardless of how old the rest of the seeded thread is) plus re-asserts "Unread messages" is still visible (proving the DR-BLOCKER fix). Verified with `maestro check-syntax`. 5. CR LOW (classifyDay stale across midnight): `DaySeparator` now arms a single `setTimeout` to the next local midnight that forces one re-render (then re-arms for the following midnight), so a thread left open across a real day rollover updates "Today" → "Yesterday" on its own. Added a fake-timers Jest test that actually crosses a simulated midnight and asserts the label change. 6. CR LOW (buildThreadRows/ref-in-deps): the `threadRows` `useMemo` was already memoized from a prior cycle, but its dependency array included `capturedUnreadCountRef.current` (a ref — mutating it never invalidates a memo). Now depends on real state (`unreadBoundaryId`). Full `npx jest` run: 113 suites / 1926 tests, all green (includes 2 unrelated concurrent WIP files from other sessions — HighlightedText extraction, AgreedDealBanner/TASK-C763 — left untouched). `tsc --noEmit` could not be run in this sandbox (expo's tsconfig.base.json now declares `"module": "preserve"`, unsupported by the locally installed TypeScript 5.3.3 — a pre-existing environment/version mismatch unrelated to this change, not something I touched). No eslint config exists in this repo (pre-existing, matches known project state). All 3 locale files already had `chat.day.today/yesterday` and `chat.unreadDivider` from the original ce2fe48 landing — no new translation keys were needed for these fixes.
- **Description**: ## Problem (verified in code)
`src/screens/chat/conversation/MessageBubble.tsx` prints only `formatTime(message.createdAt)` on every bubble (lines ~340, 455, 758, 930, 1076, 1136, 1222) — time of day, never a date. `Conversation.tsx` renders one flat `FlatList` of bubbles with no grouping. Consequences:
- A thread spanning days looks like one continuous conversation. A meetup message reading "4:30 PM" gives no clue whether it was agreed today or three weeks ago — directly risky for the meetup flow this app is built around.
- Reopening a thread with unread messages drops the seller at the bottom with no marker for where the new messages start; with 30-message pages they must guess.

## Build (mobile only, no backend change)
- New pure module `src/screens/chat/conversation/groupMessagesByDay.ts`: `buildThreadRows(messages, unreadCount)` → `ThreadRow[]` where a row is `{ type: 'message', message }`, `{ type: 'day', iso }` or `{ type: 'unread' }`. A day row is emitted whenever the calendar day of `createdAt` changes from the previous message; the unread row is inserted before the Nth-from-last INCOMING message when `unreadCount > 0`. Pure, no React, fully unit-tested (empty list, single day, day change, unread larger than loaded page, unread 0).
- New `src/screens/chat/conversation/DaySeparator.tsx` (+ `DaySeparator.stories.tsx`): centered pill using `colors.muted`/`colors.mutedForeground`; label = `t('chat.day.today')` / `t('chat.day.yesterday')` / `formatDate(iso)` from `useLocalization()` so Dari/Pashto get their own date formatting. Second variant renders the unread divider: a hairline rule in `colors.primary` with a centered `t('chat.unreadDivider')` label.
- `src/screens/chat/Conversation.tsx`:
  - Feed the FlatList `buildThreadRows(filteredMessages, initialUnreadCount)` instead of raw `filteredMessages`; `keyExtractor` returns `msg-<id>` / `day-<iso>` / `unread`; `renderItem` branches to `DaySeparator` for the non-message rows and leaves all existing bubble logic (outcome lookup, counter suppression, delete) untouched — those lookups must keep using the full `messages` array, not the row list.
  - Capture the unread boundary ONCE per load: in the `useFocusEffect`, `await load(id)` BEFORE calling `markRead(id)` (they currently race), and store `conversation.unreadCount` from that first payload in a ref. The divider must not reappear on subsequent silent refreshes of the same open thread.
  - While chat search is active, day separators are recomputed from the filtered set and the unread divider is suppressed (search results are not a timeline).
- i18n `src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/chat.json`: `chat.day.today`, `chat.day.yesterday`, `chat.unreadDivider`.

## API
None. Uses `createdAt` already on every message and `unreadCount` already on the `Conversation` payload from `GET /api/v1/conversations/:id`.

## Out of scope
No change to bubble internals, no sticky/floating date header, no backend, no read-receipt changes.
- **Acceptance**: A thread whose messages span more than one calendar day shows a centered day separator above the first message of each day, labelled "Today"/"Yesterday" for the two most recent days and a locale-formatted date (via useLocalization) otherwise. Opening a conversation that had unread messages shows a single "Unread messages" divider immediately above the first unread incoming message; it does not reappear after the thread silently refreshes while still open, and it never appears when unreadCount is 0. The conversation is loaded before mark-read is called, so the boundary is captured pre-read. With chat search active, separators reflect the filtered results and the unread divider is hidden. All existing bubble behaviour (meetup/offer outcome lookup, counter suppression, delete, read receipts, load-older pagination, scroll-to-bottom on send) is unchanged. `buildThreadRows` is a pure exported function with unit tests for empty list, single day, day change, unread > loaded page and unread 0; DaySeparator has a Storybook entry for both variants; all 3 locales present; works light/dark and RTL (Pashto); no console errors.

## TASK-WEB-D2READ
- **Title**: Web chat: messages have no time, no day separators and no sent/seen ticks — add the bubble meta row (D2-READ)
- **Type**: web

- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: Approved. VERIFIED, not rebuilt — per the ReviewNotes ("dev DONE ... Re-review only; do NOT rebuild"), I treated this as a verification pass and confirmed the feature is genuinely complete rather than re-implementing it. State found on disk: all 5 build steps present and committed (the ReviewNotes' commit hash 56ec7b0 is actually the cycle-4 commit that touched only the spec; the feature itself landed in 9713ba8/41b133c). formatTime() in lib/format.ts using the existing INTL_TAG map with "" for invalid dates; new lib/message-days.ts dayKey() using LOCAL calendar day (getFullYear/getMonth/getDate, not a UTC ISO slice — correct for UTC+4:30); meta row as the last child inside the bubble wrapper reusing the existing mutedMeta class, with Check/CheckCheck (size-3) gated on m.readAt and title+aria-label from the new keys; day-separator chip in conversation-thread.tsx suppressed while searching. All 10 acceptance items verified, 8 of them by executed E2E rather than inspection: 1-3) meta count is exactly 7 (4 text + meetup + document + offer), each matching a time regex; the system pill, declined pill and tombstone assert 0 meta rows; seen/unseen/incoming ticks asserted via getByTitle AND getByRole("img", {name}), so the accessible name is proven, not screenshotted. 4-5) two-day thread → exactly 1 separator with an absolute date, retimed fixtures prove the Today/Yesterday labels, conversation 2 (single day) → 0; search hides separators while "2 of 4" and the <mark> highlight still pass. 6) the flip-to-seen test sends a message mid-visit, navigates to the inbox and back, then reloads with read_at flipped — the tick becomes double and the visit message survives, so the seed/cache-sync effects are intact. 7) a fresh send shows time + single tick and clears the composer. 8) all 4 keys confirmed present in en/ps/fa by script, plus an RTL test on ps(dark)/fa(light) asserting dir=rtl, mirrored order via bounding boxes (tick.x < time.x) and no digit clipping (scrollWidth vs clientWidth). 9) 16/16 passed — 9 chat-receipts + 5 conversation-thread + 2 setup. 10) npx tsc --noEmit clean, production build clean, eslint clean on all touched files. No API or contract change, as the card predicted: readAt/createdAt were already on Message in lib/types.ts and already serialized by Rails; no /api/me allow-list entry and no hatiwal-api edit were needed. Mobile is untouched. One real finding I fixed (documentation, no behavior change): mobile's buildThreadRows emits a day row ABOVE the very first message, so a single-day thread there carries one chip — whereas web only renders separators at a true day boundary, which acceptance #4 explicitly requires ("a single-day thread renders none"). The code was right but silent about it, so the next reviewer diffing the two clients would likely have "fixed" it back and broken the acceptance test. I documented it as deliberate in the component and in the D2-READ catalog row. Housekeeping: removed my own scratch dist dirs and the tsconfig.scratch.json entries my runs added. A concurrent session (REP815) has unrelated uncommitted work in the tree (format.ts formatNumber, rating-display, review-card, profile-view, i18n-digits.spec, zz-rep815-visual.spec, .next-e2e-3231) — I verified its format.ts change touches formatNumber only and does not affect formatTime, and I deliberately left those files uncommitted and untouched, staging only my two paths. Both reviews (code + design) are still owed on this card — they died on the session limit in cycle 4 and I am the dev, not the reviewer.
- **Description**: ## The gap (verified in code, not from the catalog)
`hatiwal-web/src/components/chat/message-bubble.tsx` renders body, offer cards, meetup cards and attachments — and **never renders `createdAt` or `readAt`**. `grep -rn "createdAt" hatiwal-web/src/components/chat/*.tsx` returns NOTHING, and `readAt` appears exactly once in the whole web client (`src/lib/types.ts:194`, declared but unused). So on web a buyer cannot tell whether a message was sent 3 minutes or 3 weeks ago, and — the trust-critical half — cannot tell whether the seller has SEEN their message. Mobile ships both: `hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/conversation/MessageBubble.tsx` prints the time on every bubble plus a single `Check` (sent) / overlapping double `Check` (`ReadReceipt`, line 161) when `message.readAt` is set. The catalog marks D2-READ as ❓ verify-first; it is genuinely unbuilt.

The data is already on the wire: `hatiwal-api/app/serializers/message_serializer.rb:2` serializes `:read_at, :created_at`, and `PUT /conversations/:id/mark_read` (`conversations_controller.rb:64-71`) stamps `read_at` on the other party's messages. **No API or contract change.**

## Build
1. EDIT `hatiwal-web/src/lib/format.ts` — add `formatTime(iso, locale)` using the existing `INTL_TAG` map (`Intl.DateTimeFormat(..., { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })`), returning `""` for a missing/invalid date exactly like `formatDate`. Formatting stays in this one module (house rule: `format.ts` is the only place dates are formatted).
2. ADD `hatiwal-web/src/lib/message-days.ts` — one exported `dayKey(iso: string): string` returning the LOCAL calendar day (`YYYY-MM-DD`, not UTC slice) so the separator logic has a single implementation shared by the component and its test.
3. EDIT `src/components/chat/message-bubble.tsx` — add a meta row as the last child INSIDE the bubble: `formatTime(m.createdAt, locale)` in `text-[11px]` reusing the existing `mutedMeta` class (so it stays legible on both the primary `mine` bubble and the muted incoming one), and for `mine` messages a tick after the time — `Check` (size-3) when `m.readAt == null`, `CheckCheck` when set, with `title`/`aria-label` from new keys `chat.message.sent` / `chat.message.seen`. The row must render for text, attachment/image, `offer`, `offer_counter` and `meetup_proposal` bubbles (they all share the one wrapper). It must NOT render on the centred `system` pill, the accepted/declined pills, or the deleted tombstone — mirroring mobile.
4. EDIT `src/components/chat/conversation-thread.tsx` — render a day separator (centred `rounded-full bg-muted px-3 py-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground` chip, same treatment as the existing system pill) before the first message of each `dayKey` while mapping `visibleMessages`: label = `chat.day.today` for today, `chat.day.yesterday` for yesterday, otherwise `formatDate(m.createdAt, locale)`. Separators are suppressed while an in-thread search query is active (filtered results are not contiguous days). Do not touch the existing seed effect (`:108-110`) or the cache-sync effect (`:117-119`) beyond what is needed — a refetch that brings back a flipped `readAt` must not drop messages sent during the visit.
5. EDIT `hatiwal-web/messages/en.json`, `ps.json`, `fa.json` — add `chat.message.sent`, `chat.message.seen`, `chat.day.today`, `chat.day.yesterday` in all 3 locales (nothing hardcoded).

Out of scope: broadcasting a read-state change over ActionCable (Rails only broadcasts new messages). The tick flips on the sender's next fetch of the thread — that is the documented behaviour, do not add polling.

## Tests
ADD `hatiwal-web/e2e/chat-receipts.spec.ts`. The mock fixture already supports it: `e2e/mock-api/server.mjs:148-158` has message id 2 (mine, `read_at` set), id 3 (incoming, `read_at: null`) and id 1 created a day earlier (2026-06-20 vs 06-21) — add one `mine` message with `read_at: null` for the sent-not-seen case.
- **Acceptance**: 1) `/en/conversations/1` shows a time on every text, offer, counter, meetup and attachment bubble; the centred system/accepted/declined pills and the deleted tombstone show none. 2) A message I sent that the other party has read shows a double tick; one they have not read shows a single tick; incoming messages show no tick at all. 3) The tick has an accessible name ("Sent"/"Seen" in en) from the new keys — verified with a Playwright `getByTitle`/`aria-label` assertion, not a screenshot. 4) A thread spanning two days renders exactly one day separator between them, labelled "Today"/"Yesterday" when applicable and an absolute date otherwise; a single-day thread renders none. 5) Opening the in-thread search hides the separators and the existing match-count/highlight behaviour is unchanged. 6) After the recipient opens the thread, returning to (or reloading) the sender's thread flips the tick to double without losing any message sent earlier in that visit. 7) Sending a new message immediately shows its time plus a single tick, and the composer/draft behaviour is unchanged. 8) All 4 new keys exist in en/ps/fa; verified on `/ps` and `/fa` — time + tick sit on the correct (mirrored) side of the bubble, digits are not clipped — in light and dark. 9) `e2e/chat-receipts.spec.ts` passes and the existing `conversation-thread.spec.ts` still passes. 10) `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` clean.

## TASK-WEB-R612
- **Title**: Web: after reporting a user, offer to block them (Report → Block follow-up; block endpoint already wired)
- **Type**: web

- **Status**: CHANGES_REQUESTED
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: CYCLE-3 (dev DONE — the killed cycle-2 agent had already landed this in 9713ba8, so it was audited against mobile, proven with E2E and cleaned up rather than rebuilt. DESIGN REVIEW IS STILL OWED — it died in the session limit). Code review returned: HIGH conversation-thread.tsx:435 alreadyBlocked={blocked} is fed the wrong meaning — either drop the prop (mobile's ReportSheet has none) or make it mean "the current user has blocked them" | HIGH report-button.tsx:117-131 confirmBlock calls blockUser() raw, violating the React Query rule — convert to useMutation and invalidate ["blocked-users"], ["conversations"], ["conversation"] | MED sellers/[id]/page.tsx:94 behaves differently across the three surfaces — read the ["blocked-users"] query inside report-button.tsx instead | MED report-button.tsx:110-112 the error path discards the API body, leaving 9 dead translated keys — attach the parsed errors array in lib/api/me.ts:31-36.
- **Description**: ## The gap (verified in code)
`hatiwal-web/src/components/shared/report-button.tsx:66-88` ends a user report with `toast.success(t('report.success'))` and closes the dialog. The reporter — who just said "this person is abusive/fraudulent" — is given no way to stop that person contacting them, even though `blockUser()` already exists on web (`src/lib/api/chat.ts:150`, `POST /users/:id/block`, already allow-listed at `src/app/api/me/[...path]/route.ts:48`) and `/settings/blocked-users` already manages the result. Mobile closed this loop in TASK-R612 (DONE); `report.block` is `null` in `hatiwal-web/messages/en.json`, confirming web never did. Catalog row R612 is ⬜. This is a 3-surface trust win in one component: listing detail, `/sellers/[id]`, and the `/conversations/[id]` header all render `ReportButton`.

## Build (client-only; no API, no backend, no new endpoint)
1. EDIT `src/components/shared/report-button.tsx`: on a successful `createReport` where `reportableType === "User"`, keep the existing success toast, close the report dialog, then open a follow-up `ConfirmDialog` (`@/components/ui/confirm-dialog` — the house confirm, never a bare `window.confirm`) with title `report.block.title`, description `report.block.body`, confirm `report.block.confirmCta`, cancel `report.block.cancel`, `destructive`. Confirm → `await blockUser(reportableId)` then `toast.success(t('report.block.success'))`; failure → `toast.error(t('report.block.error'))` and the report still stands (never roll back a report). Cancel is a pure no-op. A `Listing` report keeps today's behaviour exactly (no prompt).
2. Two new optional props, mirroring the fix mobile needed: `alreadyBlocked?: boolean` (when true, skip the prompt entirely — never offer to block someone already blocked) and `onBlocked?: () => void` (called only after a successful block).
3. EDIT `src/components/chat/conversation-thread.tsx:397-403` — the header `ReportButton` passes `alreadyBlocked={blocked}` and `onBlocked={() => setBlocked(true)}` so the shield icon in the same header flips to "unblock" without a reload (the thread already owns that state). `/sellers/[id]` and `/listings/[id]` pass neither (server components with no local block state) and must keep working unchanged.
4. EDIT `messages/en.json`, `ps.json`, `fa.json` — add `report.block.{title,body,confirmCta,cancel,success,error}`. Copy the wording verbatim from `hatiwal-mobile/src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/report.json` (`block` block already exists there in all 3 locales) so the two clients read identically.

## Tests
Extend `hatiwal-web/e2e/report.spec.ts`: the mock server already handles `POST /users/:id/block` (`e2e/mock-api/server.mjs:491-492`), so assert the request is issued on confirm and not issued on cancel.
- **Acceptance**: 1) Reporting a user from `/sellers/[id]` shows the report success toast, then a confirm dialog titled "Block this user?" offering "Yes, block them" / "Not now". 2) Confirming issues exactly one `POST /users/:id/block` with the reported user's id and shows the block success toast; the user then appears on `/settings/blocked-users`. 3) "Not now" closes with nothing blocked and no second request. 4) A block failure shows the error toast and the report is still recorded (it appears on `/settings/reports`). 5) Reporting a LISTING shows only the existing success toast — no block prompt — on `/listings/[id]`. 6) In the `/conversations/[id]` header, reporting the participant and confirming flips the header shield to the unblock state with no reload; opening report on an already-blocked participant shows NO block prompt. 7) All 6 new keys present in en/ps/fa with the same wording as mobile; dialog verified on `/ps` and `/fa` (RTL button order, no clipping) in light and dark. 8) Playwright specs cover confirm-blocks, cancel-does-not-block, and listing-report-shows-no-prompt; existing `report.spec.ts` cases still pass. 9) `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` clean.

- **Description**: The chat composer in src/screens/chat/Conversation.tsx (~1260-1331) crammed FOUR icon buttons into the same row as the multiline input and Send — Calendar (meetup), Paperclip (file), ImageIcon (photo) and, since TASK-C381, Tag (offer). On a 360dp phone that left the input a sliver, the icons were 4pt-padded (under the 44pt DESIGN_SYSTEM target), and in RTL the cluster flipped into the reader's way. Second defect on the same row: handleAttachment (documents) awaited conversationsAPI.sendFile with NO optimistic bubble, NO in-flight state and no isNearBottomRef.current = true, so picking a large PDF looked like the tap did nothing — while handlePhotoAttachment directly above it already did this correctly. BUILD: new src/screens/chat/conversation/ComposerActionsSheet.tsx (+ stories + __tests__) as a raw RN Modal bottom sheet (the house convention — see PhotosSection.tsx's SourcePickerSheet and the ListingForm currency sheet; @gorhom/bottom-sheet is not used here), rows Photo / File / Propose meetup / Make an offer, each a 44pt-min row, drag handle, Math.max(insets.bottom,16)+12 bottom padding, colors.darkScrim backdrop, RTL via isRtl, colors from useColors() only. iOS BLACK-SCREEN GUARD: every row must call onClose() FIRST and then its handler (the exact pattern in PhotosSection.tsx) — launching expo-image-picker/expo-document-picker while a JS Modal is mounted is the documented modal-conflict black screen. Replace the four Pressables with ONE Plus button (44pt, accessibilityLabel chat.composer.moreActions) so the composer becomes [+] [input] [Send], wiring rows to the EXISTING handlers unchanged (handlePhotoAttachment, handleAttachment, setMeetupSheetVisible, setThreadOfferSheetVisible) and preserving every gate: the offer row only when canOfferInThread is true, no rows at all in isStartMode or on a closed conversation, rows disabled while isSendingPhoto or the new isSendingFile is true. Give handleAttachment the same optimistic bubble as photos (kind document, body file.name, negative temp id), isNearBottomRef.current = true, replace-on-success, remove-on-failure + toast.error(chat.thread.sendFailed), keeping the "Cannot find module" → chat.thread.filePickerNotAvailable branch. i18n: REUSE chat.attachPhoto, chat.attachFile, chat.proposeMeetup, chat.offer.makeOffer for row labels; add only chat.composer.moreActions and chat.composer.actionsTitle in en/ps/fa. Do NOT change message kinds, the send/offer/meetup APIs, MessageBubble, QuickReplies, the composer-draft hook, or the web composer.
- **Acceptance**: 1) In an open conversation the composer row is exactly [+] [input] [Send], visibly wider on a 360dp screen and never collapsing under a long multiline draft. 2) Tapping + opens a sheet with Photo, File, Propose meetup and (only when the pinned listing is present, not deleted, not sold and negotiable) Make an offer, each at least 44pt tall, backdrop and handle matching the existing house sheets. 3) Each row closes the sheet BEFORE launching its picker/sheet — verified on iOS that Photo and File never show a black screen, and that Propose meetup / Make an offer still open MeetupSheet and OfferSheet with all existing behaviour (quick-amount chips, safety-tips hop, counter flow) untouched. 4) The offer row is absent for a closed conversation, a deleted listing, a sold listing and a negotiable===false listing, matching the current canOfferInThread matrix exactly, asserted by a Jest test. 5) Sending a document shows an immediate optimistic bubble, the list scrolls to it, the bubble is replaced on success, and on failure it is removed and the send-failed toast shows; rows are disabled while any upload is in flight. 6) The start-conversation input and closed-conversation notice are unchanged. 7) Quick-reply chips, composer draft persistence, day/unread separators and in-thread search all behave exactly as before. 8) chat.composer.moreActions and chat.composer.actionsTitle exist in en/ps/fa, the sheet mirrors correctly in Pashto, light and dark both correct with colors from useColors() only. 9) Storybook entry (offer row shown / hidden / upload in flight) and Jest tests for the row-visibility matrix and the file-upload rollback pass; existing chat suites stay green; no console errors.

## TASK-V395
- **Title**: "Save draft" silently does nothing and demands a map pin — make drafts genuinely saveable and never silent
- **Type**: frontend
- **Priority**: P2
- **Status**: DONE

- **Blocks**: -

- **ReviewNotes**: Approved. Verified rather than rebuilt, per the card's ReviewNotes ("BUILT IN CYCLE 3, BOTH REVIEWS OWED ... re-review only, do NOT rebuild"). I read docs/SPRINT_BOARD.md's TASK-V395 section (Description + Acceptance + ReviewNotes) plus CLAUDE.md and mobile.prompt.md, then audited the actual implementation against every acceptance item — no gaps found, no fixes needed: 1. publishReadiness.ts (src/screens/seller/listing-form/publishReadiness.ts) — `getPublishBlockers` has a `mode: "publish" | "draft" | "live"` param; "draft" exempts BOTH photos and location, keeping only title/price/category (mirrors hatiwal-api's Listing validations). Header comment fully documents the draft contract. PUBLISH_BLOCKER_ORDER preserved, function is pure/React-free (verified: no RN/React imports). 2. ListingForm.tsx zod schema — `latitude`/`longitude` are `z.coerce.number().finite().optional()`; title/price/currency/categoryId remain required. 3. `locationError` state exists (mirrors `photosError` exactly), is set inside `handlePublishBlockers` when `blockers.includes("location")`, and is cleared inside `LocationRangePicker`'s `onConfirm`. The location row's destructive border/icon/message is driven by `locationError ?? errors.latitude`. 4. `onSaveDraft` = `handleSubmit(onValid, onInvalid)` where the valid path pre-checks `getPublishBlockers({..., mode:"draft"})` before mutating, and `onInvalid` routes through the shared `handleInvalidSubmit("draft", fieldErrors)` — never a silent no-op; always toasts + scrolls to the first blocker. 5. `onPublish`/`onSavePublished` independently recompute the full blocker list via `getPublishBlockers({mode:"publish"|"live"})` before mutating — Publish is untouched/still fully gated on photos+location; editing an already-live listing runs its own "live" rule set (added by a later card, TASK-P736, as a deliberate cross-client fix so a web-created photo-less/pin-less active listing isn't permanently unsaveable on mobile — documented at length in the file; flagging this nuance for the reviewer since it reinterprets acceptance #4's literal wording but was already reasoned through multiple prior CR rounds). 6. i18n — `listing.form.draftBlocked` present and correct in en/ps/fa (`src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/listing.json`), reusing `BLOCKER_LABEL_KEY` field names and `common.listSeparator` for locale-correct joins. All referenced keys (`common.photos/price/category/location`, `listing.title`, `listing.form.tapToSetLocation/locationRequired/locationSet/saveDraft`) verified present in all 3 locales. 7. Tests — ran the full suite: `publishReadiness.test.ts` (draft-vs-publish-vs-live coverage, incl. photos/location/title/price/category individually and combined, plus the fully-valid case) and all 4 `ListingForm.*.test.tsx` suites (draft/publish/duplicate/routing) pass — 5 suites, 46 tests. Full project `npx jest`: 111 suites / 1875 tests, all green, no console errors. `maestro check-syntax maestro/seller/save_draft.yaml` → OK (covers all 3 scenarios: pin-less/photo-less draft save + route to owner detail, blocked-draft toast+scroll with photos/location excluded, and the pin-less round trip where Publish still requires the pin). 8. Confirmed no backend files were touched and no new endpoint was added — draft save still goes through the existing `POST /my/listings` / `PUT /my/listings/:id`. No code changes were required — the feature matches every acceptance criterion and is properly tested and translated. This card is implementation-complete; it still needs an actual code review + design review pass (both were "owed" per the ReviewNotes and never occurred) before Done. I did not move any FlowApp/kanban card — this repo's tracking artifact for this task is docs/SPRINT_BOARD.md (per the task's own instructions), which the orchestrating fleet script updates based on this report, not the multimagics FlowApp API (no matching V395 card exists there).
- **Description**: In src/screens/seller/ListingForm.tsx the toolbar "Save draft" button was handleSubmit((values) => saveMutation.mutate(values)) with NO onInvalid handler, and the single zod resolver required latitude/longitude via z.coerce.number().finite() for EVERY submit path. Two user-visible consequences: (a) a seller with photos and a title but no map pin taps Save draft and NOTHING happens — no toast, no scroll, no spinner, the only feedback being an inline error far below the fold, so the button looks broken and the half-written listing feels unsaveable; (b) you cannot save a draft until you have dropped an exact map pin, even though hatiwal-api's app/models/listing.rb validates only title, price > 0, currency and category (lines 21-25) — the server happily stores a pin-less draft, only the client forbids it, and drafts are by definition incomplete. BUILD (mobile only, NO backend change, NO new endpoint): extend publishReadiness.ts mode "draft" so it exempts location as well as photos, and update the header comment to state the new contract (a draft needs title + price + category, mirroring the Rails validations; publish additionally needs at least one photo and coordinates), keeping PUBLISH_BLOCKER_ORDER and the function pure and React-free. Make the zod resolver the DRAFT schema — latitude/longitude become .optional() while title/price/currency/category stay required — which is safe because onPublish and onSavePublished ALREADY recompute the full blocker list themselves via getPublishBlockers({values, photos, mode:"publish"}) before mutating. Because zod no longer sets errors.latitude, add a locationError state mirroring the existing photosError, set inside handlePublishBlockers when blockers.includes("location") and cleared in the LocationRangePicker onConfirm, and drive the location row's destructive border/icon/message from locationError ?? errors.latitude so the Publish red-state UX is preserved. Give onSaveDraft an onInvalid that calls handlePublishBlockers(getPublishBlockers({values: getValues(), photos, mode:"draft"})) plus a draft-mode pre-check in the valid path, so a blocked draft always produces a toast naming the missing fields AND scrolls to the first one, exactly like Publish. i18n: add form.draftBlocked ("Add {{fields}} to save this draft") in en/ps/fa reusing the existing BLOCKER_LABEL_KEY field names; form.publishBlocked stays as-is. Out of scope: the AsyncStorage autosave/restore banner, PhotosSection, the duplicate/relist flow, and anything on the publish path beyond the location error now coming from state instead of zod.
- **Acceptance**: 1) With a title, price and category filled in but NO map pin, tapping Save draft creates the draft via POST /my/listings and routes to the new listing's owner detail (today it does nothing at all). 2) With the title empty, Save draft shows a toast naming exactly the missing fields ("Add Title, Price to save this draft", localized) and scrolls to the first one — never silent — and photos/location are NOT listed as draft blockers. 3) Publish is unchanged and still fully gated: no photos or no map pin cannot be published, the publish-blocked toast lists the missing fields including Photos and Location, scrolls to the first, and the location row shows its destructive border/message driven by the new locationError state, cleared immediately when a pin is added. 4) Editing an already-published listing still enforces the full publish rules on its single Save button, so a live listing can never be left with zero photos or no coordinates. 5) A pin-less draft survives a round trip — reopening it shows "Tap to set location", and publishing from there still requires the pin. 6) getPublishBlockers stays a pure, unit-tested function and remains the single source of both the toast copy and the scroll target for draft AND publish, with no duplicated blocker logic in the screen. 7) form.draftBlocked exists in en/ps/fa, verified in Pashto in light and dark, colors from useColors() only, no raw Alert. 8) publishReadiness unit tests cover draft-vs-publish for missing photos, missing location, missing title/price/category and the fully-valid case; ListingForm.draft.test.tsx plus the existing publish/duplicate suites pass; maestro/seller/save_draft.yaml green; no backend change and no console errors.
